    13	import { completionResponse, errorResponse, startFakeVendor } from '../llm/fake-vendor.ts';
    14	import { clientFor } from '../gm/support.ts';
    15	import { ROOMS, SUSPECTS, WEAPONS } from '../../src/engine/cards.ts';
    16	
    17	const OFFLINE = { seed: 1, players: 3, useLlm: false } as const;
    18	
    19	/** Every card named on the "Your hand:" line the loop printed for the player. */
    20	function handFrom(lines: readonly string[]): string[] {
    21	  const line = lines.find((candidate) => candidate.startsWith('Your hand: '));
    22	  if (line === undefined) throw new Error('the loop never printed the player\'s hand');
    23	  return line.slice('Your hand: '.length).split(', ');
    24	}
    25	
    26	describe('a full offline game', () => {
    27	  test('reaches game over from a fixed seed, with no client and no network', async () => {
    28	    const session = drivenSession(plainPlayer());
    29	    // No `createClient` at all: there is nothing in this run that COULD make a
    30	    // request, which is what --no-llm has to mean.
    31	    const code = await runGame(OFFLINE, { io: session.io });
    32	    const text = session.text();
    33	
    34	    expect(code).toBe(0);
    35	    expect(text).toContain('Seed 1 · 3 at the table · offline (--no-llm)');
    36	    expect(text).toContain('bun run src/cli.ts --seed 1 --players 3');
    37	    // The canned scenario is used verbatim when there is no game master.
    38	    expect(text).toContain('The dead: Doctor Alastair Vane');
    39	    expect(text).toContain('── Turn 1 — you are Miss Scarlett');
    40	    expect(text).toContain('── Case closed ──');
    41	    expect(text).toMatch(/The answer: .+ in the .+ with the .+\./);
    42	    expect(text).toContain('No LLM calls were made this session.');
    43	    expect(session.answers.length).toBeGreaterThan(10);
    44	  });
    45	
    46	  test('the same seed and the same answers replay exactly', async () => {
    47	    const first = drivenSession(plainPlayer());
    48	    await runGame(OFFLINE, { io: first.io });
    49	    const second = drivenSession(plainPlayer());
    50	    await runGame(OFFLINE, { io: second.io });
    51	
    52	    expect(second.text()).toBe(first.text());
    53	    expect(second.answers).toEqual(first.answers);
    54	  });
    55	
    56	  test('a different seed is a different game', async () => {
    57	    const first = drivenSession(plainPlayer());
    58	    await runGame(OFFLINE, { io: first.io });
    59	    const other = drivenSession(plainPlayer());
    60	    await runGame({ ...OFFLINE, seed: 2 }, { io: other.io });
    61	
    62	    expect(other.text()).not.toBe(first.text());
    63	    expect(other.text()).toContain('── Case closed ──');
    64	  });
    65	
    66	  test('the answer is revealed only at the end, and only once', async () => {
    67	    const session = drivenSession(plainPlayer());
    68	    await runGame(OFFLINE, { io: session.io });
    69	
    70	    const reveals = session.lines.filter((line) => line.startsWith('The answer: '));
    71	    expect(reveals).toHaveLength(1);
    72	    expect(session.lines.indexOf(reveals[0] as string)).toBeGreaterThan(
    73	      session.lines.indexOf('── Case closed ──'),
    74	    );
    75	  });
    76	
    77	  test('four, five and six seats all play to a finish', async () => {
    78	    for (const players of [4, 5, 6]) {
    79	      const session = drivenSession(plainPlayer());
    80	      const code = await runGame({ seed: 7, players, useLlm: false }, { io: session.io });
    81	      expect(code).toBe(0);
    82	      expect(session.text()).toContain('── Case closed ──');
    83	    }
    84	  }, 30_000);
    85	});
    86	
    87	describe('the refutation the player owes', () => {
    88	  /**
    89	   * Seed 2 puts the player in front of a choice: a suggestion they can answer
    90	   * with either of two cards. The engine parks in `awaiting-refutation` and the
    91	   * loop must be the thing that asks.
    92	   */
    93	  test('offers only cards from the player\'s own hand, and play continues after the choice', async () => {
    94	    const menus: string[][] = [];
    95	    const player = plainPlayer();
    96	    const driver: Driver = (lines, promptText) => {
    97	      if (lastTitle(lines).includes('you must show ONE of these')) menus.push(lastMenu(lines));
    98	      return player(lines, promptText);
    99	    };
   100	
   101	    const session = drivenSession(driver);
   102	    await runGame({ seed: 2, players: 3, useLlm: false }, { io: session.io });
   103	    const text = session.text();
   104	
   105	    expect(menus.length).toBeGreaterThan(0);
   106	    const hand = handFrom(session.lines);
   107	    const offered = (menus[0] as string[]).map((line) => line.replace(/^ {2}\d+\) /, ''));
   108	    expect(offered.length).toBeGreaterThan(1);
   109	    for (const card of offered) expect(hand).toContain(card);
   110	
   111	    // The choice was made, the table learned only THAT it happened, and the
   112	    // game moved on rather than deadlocking on the pending refutation.
   113	    expect(text).toContain(`${HUMAN_SEAT} refutes`);
   114	    expect(text).toContain('── Case closed ──');
   115	  });
   116	
   117	  test('a suggestion the player cannot answer is never turned into a prompt', async () => {
   118	    // p1 makes suggestions of its own all game; the loop must only ask for a
   119	    // card when the ENGINE says this seat owes one.
   120	    const session = drivenSession(plainPlayer());
   121	    await runGame(OFFLINE, { io: session.io });
   122	
   123	    const prompts = session.lines.filter((line) => line.includes('you must show ONE of these'));
   124	    const refusals = session.lines.filter((line) =>
   125	      line.startsWith('That will not work: ') && line.includes('refutation'),
   126	    );
   127	    expect(refusals).toHaveLength(0);
   128	    for (const line of prompts) expect(line).toContain('waiting');
   129	  });
   130	});
   131	
   132	describe('bad input', () => {
   133	  test('an answer that means nothing is reprinted, not fatal', async () => {
   134	    const player = plainPlayer();
   135	    let injected = false;
   136	    const driver: Driver = (lines, promptText) => {
   137	      if (!injected) {
   138	        injected = true;
   139	        return 'banana';
   140	      }
   141	      return player(lines, promptText);
   142	    };
   143	
   144	    const session = drivenSession(driver);
   145	    const code = await runGame(OFFLINE, { io: session.io });
   146	
   147	    expect(session.text()).toContain('I do not understand "banana"');
   148	    // The same menu is offered again: the first two prompts are the same block.
   149	    expect(session.lines.filter((line) => line === 'What will you do?').length).toBeGreaterThan(1);
   150	    expect(code).toBe(0);
   151	    expect(session.text()).toContain('── Case closed ──');
   152	  });
   153	
   154	  test('a legal word for an illegal move is refused in the engine\'s own words', async () => {
   155	    const player = plainPlayer();
   156	    let injected = false;
   157	    const driver: Driver = (lines, promptText) => {
   158	      if (!injected) {
   159	        injected = true;
   160	        // Turn 1: the player is in a corridor and has not rolled. "suggest" is
   161	        // a real command, but not a legal one here.
   162	        return 'suggest';
   163	      }
   164	      return player(lines, promptText);
   165	    };
   166	
   167	    const session = drivenSession(driver);
   168	    const code = await runGame(OFFLINE, { io: session.io });
   169	
   170	    expect(session.text()).toContain('That will not work: p1 must be in a room to suggest');
   171	    expect(code).toBe(0);
   172	    expect(session.text()).toContain('── Case closed ──');
   173	  });
   174	
   175	  test('closing stdin ends the session and still prints the ledger', async () => {
   176	    const session = drivenSession(scriptedDriver([]));
   177	    const code = await runGame(OFFLINE, { io: session.io });
   178	
   179	    expect(code).toBe(0);
   180	    expect(session.text()).toContain('You leave the house with the case unsolved.');
   181	    expect(session.text()).toContain('No LLM calls were made this session.');
   182	    expect(session.text()).not.toContain('The answer:');
   183	  });
   184	
   185	  test('quit leaves without revealing the answer', async () => {
   186	    const session = drivenSession(scriptedDriver(['quit']));
   187	    const code = await runGame(OFFLINE, { io: session.io });
   188	
   189	    expect(code).toBe(0);
   190	    expect(session.answers).toEqual(['quit']);
   191	    expect(session.text()).toContain('You leave the house with the case unsolved.');
   192	    expect(session.text()).not.toContain('The answer:');
   193	  });
   194	});
   195	
   196	describe('the notebook', () => {
   197	  test('shows what the seat can prove and nothing it cannot', async () => {
   198	    const session = drivenSession(scriptedDriver(['notes', 'quit']));
   199	    await runGame(OFFLINE, { io: session.io });
   200	    const text = session.text();
   201	
   202	    expect(text).toContain('Notebook — turn 1');
   203	    expect(text).toContain('Proven in another hand: (nothing yet)');
   204	    // Before anything has been shown, every card the seat does not hold is open.
   205	    const hand = handFrom(session.lines);
   206	    const open = text.slice(text.indexOf('Suspects still possible: '));
   207	    for (const card of [...SUSPECTS, ...WEAPONS, ...ROOMS]) {
   208	      if (hand.includes(card)) expect(open).not.toContain(`${card},`);
   209	    }
   210	  });
   211	});
   212	
   213	describe('with a game master', () => {
   214	  test('narrates in the model\'s words and prints the measured token ledger', async () => {
   215	    const vendor = startFakeVendor((request) => {
   216	      const body = request.body as { messages: { content: string }[] };
   217	      const prompt = body.messages.map((message) => message.content).join('\n');
   218	      if (prompt.includes('Return exactly this JSON shape')) {
   219	        return completionResponse({
   220	          content: JSON.stringify({
   221	            victim: 'Lord Edgemere',
   222	            setting: 'A rain-locked manor',
   223	            intro: 'The clock stopped at nine.',
   224	            suspects: [{ name: 'Miss Scarlett', persona: 'All silk and no answers.' }],
   225	          }),
   226	          model: 'Test-Model',
   227	          usage: { prompt_tokens: 100, completion_tokens: 20, total_tokens: 120 },
   228	        });
   229	      }
   230	      // Narration: one string per numbered line requested.
   231	      const count = (prompt.match(/^\d+\. /gm) ?? []).length;
   232	      return completionResponse({
   233	        content: JSON.stringify(Array.from({ length: count }, (_u, index) => `Narrated line ${index + 1}.`)),
   234	        model: 'Test-Model',
   235	        usage: { prompt_tokens: 30, completion_tokens: 10, total_tokens: 40 },
   236	      });
   237	    });
   238	
   239	    try {
   240	      const session = drivenSession(scriptedDriver(['quit']));
   241	      const client = clientFor(vendor.baseUrl);
   242	      await runGame({ seed: 1, players: 3, useLlm: true }, { io: session.io, createClient: () => client });
   243	      const text = session.text();
   244	
   245	      expect(text).toContain('Lord Edgemere');
   246	      expect(text).toContain('game master: Test-Model');
   247	      expect(text).toContain('Narrated line 1.');
   248	      // The ledger is read from the responses' own usage objects, per model.
   249	      expect(text).toContain('LLM usage: 2 calls, 160 tokens (130 prompt + 30 completion)');
   250	      expect(text).toContain('  test-model: 2 calls, 160 tokens');
   251	      expect(vendor.requests.length).toBe(2);
   252	    } finally {
   253	      await vendor.stop();
   254	    }
   255	  });
   256	
   257	  test('a vendor that fails is one line of notice, and the game plays on', async () => {
   258	    const vendor = startFakeVendor(() => errorResponse(500, 'down'));
   259	    try {
   260	      const session = drivenSession(plainPlayer());
   261	      const client = clientFor(vendor.baseUrl, { retryBackoffMs: 1 });
   262	      const code = await runGame(
   263	        { seed: 2, players: 3, useLlm: true },
   264	        { io: session.io, createClient: () => client },
   265	      );
   266	      const text = session.text();
   267	
   268	      expect(code).toBe(0);
   269	      expect(text).toContain('the game master is not answering');
   270	      // Said once, however many calls fail.
   271	      expect(session.lines.filter((line) => line.includes('the game master is not answering'))).toHaveLength(1);
   272	      // The canned scenario and the engine's own sentences carry the game.
   273	      expect(text).toContain('The dead: Doctor Alastair Vane');
   274	      expect(text).toContain('── Case closed ──');
   275	      // Every call failed, so there is nothing honest to report as usage.
   276	      expect(text).toContain('No LLM calls were made this session.');
   277	    } finally {
   278	      await vendor.stop();
   279	    }
   280	  }, 30_000);
   281	
   282	  test('a client that cannot even be built is a notice, not a crash', async () => {
   283	    const session = drivenSession(scriptedDriver(['quit']));
   284	    const code = await runGame(
   285	      { seed: 1, players: 3, useLlm: true },
   286	      {
   287	        io: session.io,
   288	        createClient: () => {
   289	          throw new Error('Missing POE_API_KEY.');
   290	        },
   291	      },
   292	    );
   293	
   294	    expect(code).toBe(0);
   295	    expect(session.text()).toContain('(no game master: Missing POE_API_KEY.)');
   296	    expect(session.text()).toContain('playing offline');
   297	    expect(session.text()).toContain('No LLM calls were made this session.');
   298	  });
   299	});
   300	
   301	describe('resolveChoice', () => {
   302	  const options = [
   303	    { key: 'roll', label: 'roll', value: 'roll' },
   304	    { key: 'notes', label: 'notes', value: 'notes' },
   305	    { key: 'quit', label: 'quit', value: 'quit' },
   306	  ];
   307	
   308	  test.each([
   309	    ['1', 'roll'],
   310	    ['3', 'quit'],
   311	    ['roll', 'roll'],
   312	    ['ROLL', 'roll'],
   313	    ['  quit  ', 'quit'],
   314	    ['q', 'quit'],
   315	  ])('%s resolves to %s', (answer, expected) => {
   316	    expect(resolveChoice(answer, options)?.value).toBe(expected);
   317	  });
   318	
   319	  test.each([['0'], ['4'], [''], ['banana'], ['zz']])('%s resolves to nothing', (answer) => {
   320	    expect(resolveChoice(answer, options)).toBeNull();
   321	  });
   322	
   323	  test('an ambiguous prefix resolves to nothing rather than to a guess', () => {
   324	    const ambiguous = [...options, { key: 'nudge', label: 'nudge', value: 'nudge' }];
   325	    expect(resolveChoice('n', ambiguous)).toBeNull();
   326	    expect(resolveChoice('no', ambiguous)?.value).toBe('notes');
   327	  });
   328	});
     1	/**
     2	 * The entry point as the run contract sees it: a real `bun run src/cli.ts`
     3	 * process, with real stdio.
     4	 *
     5	 * This is the only test that exercises `createStdio` and the exit codes, and it
     6	 * is the healthcheck the project publishes — if `--help` stops exiting 0, this
     7	 * fails.
     8	 */
     9	
    10	import { describe, expect, test } from 'bun:test';
    11	import { mkdtempSync } from 'node:fs';
    12	import { tmpdir } from 'node:os';
    13	import { join, resolve } from 'node:path';
    14	
    15	const CLI = resolve(import.meta.dir, '../../src/cli.ts');
    16	
    17	async function run(
    18	  args: readonly string[],
    19	  stdin = '',
    20	  cwd?: string,
    21	): Promise<{ code: number; stdout: string; stderr: string }> {
    22	  const child = Bun.spawn(['bun', 'run', CLI, ...args], {
    23	    stdin: new TextEncoder().encode(stdin),
    24	    stdout: 'pipe',
    25	    stderr: 'pipe',
    26	    ...(cwd === undefined ? {} : { cwd }),
    27	    // No key in the environment. `loadLlmConfig` also reads .env.local from the
    28	    // CWD, so a test that must be keyless runs from somewhere without one —
    29	    // otherwise the maintainer's real key would send this suite to the vendor.
    30	    env: { ...process.env, POE_API_KEY: '' },
    31	  });
    32	  const [stdout, stderr, code] = await Promise.all([
    33	    new Response(child.stdout).text(),
    34	    new Response(child.stderr).text(),
    35	    child.exited,
    36	  ]);
    37	  return { code, stdout, stderr };
    38	}
    39	
    40	describe('bun run src/cli.ts', () => {
    41	  test('--help prints the usage and exits 0 (the healthcheck)', async () => {
    42	    const { code, stdout } = await run(['--help']);
    43	    expect(code).toBe(0);
    44	    expect(stdout).toContain('Usage: bun run src/cli.ts');
    45	    expect(stdout).toContain('--no-llm');
    46	  }, 30_000);
    47	
    48	  test('-h is the same door', async () => {
    49	    const { code, stdout } = await run(['-h']);
    50	    expect(code).toBe(0);
    51	    expect(stdout).toContain('Usage: bun run src/cli.ts');
    52	  }, 30_000);
    53	
    54	  test('a seat count out of bounds is refused on stderr with exit 2', async () => {
    55	    const { code, stdout, stderr } = await run(['--players', '2']);
    56	    expect(code).toBe(2);
    57	    expect(stderr).toContain('--players must be a whole number between 3 and 6');
    58	    expect(stderr).toContain('--help');
    59	    expect(stdout).toBe('');
    60	  }, 30_000);
    61	
    62	  test('an unknown flag is refused rather than ignored', async () => {
    63	    const { code, stderr } = await run(['--turbo']);
    64	    expect(code).toBe(2);
    65	    expect(stderr).toContain('Unknown option: --turbo');
    66	  }, 30_000);
    67	
    68	  test('a whole offline game plays over real stdin and ends with the ledger', async () => {
    69	    // Always answer "1": roll, take the first destination, suggest, show the
    70	    // first card. It never accuses, so the game is finished by the suspects.
    71	    const answers = `${'1\n'.repeat(600)}`;
    72	    const { code, stdout } = await run(['--no-llm', '--seed', '2', '--players', '3'], answers);
    73	
    74	    expect(code).toBe(0);
    75	    expect(stdout).toContain('Seed 2 · 3 at the table · offline (--no-llm)');
    76	    expect(stdout).toContain('── Case closed ──');
    77	    expect(stdout).toMatch(/The answer: .+ in the .+ with the .+\./);
    78	    expect(stdout).toContain('No LLM calls were made this session.');
    79	  }, 60_000);
    80	
    81	  test('closing stdin immediately still leaves cleanly', async () => {
    82	    const { code, stdout } = await run(['--no-llm', '--seed', '3'], '');
    83	    expect(code).toBe(0);
    84	    expect(stdout).toContain('You leave the house with the case unsolved.');
    85	  }, 30_000);
    86	
    87	  test('without a key the game still starts, offline, saying so once', async () => {
    88	    // No --no-llm: the client cannot be built, and that is a notice, not a
    89	    // crash. Run from a directory with no .env.local so no key can be found.
    90	    const { code, stdout } = await run(['--seed', '3'], 'quit\n', mkdtempSync(join(tmpdir(), 'mm-cli-')));
    91	    expect(code).toBe(0);
    92	    expect(stdout).toContain('(no game master: Missing POE_API_KEY');
    93	    expect(stdout).toContain('playing offline');
    94	    expect(stdout).toContain('No LLM calls were made this session.');
    95	  }, 30_000);
    96	});
     1	/**
     2	 * Argument parsing for the terminal entry point.
     3	 *
     4	 * `--help` is the run contract's healthcheck, so its shape is asserted here as
     5	 * well as end to end in cli.test.ts: it must exit 0 and actually document the
     6	 * flags, not merely print something.
     7	 */
     8	
     9	import { describe, expect, test } from 'bun:test';
    10	import { parseArgs, USAGE } from '../../src/ui/args.ts';
    11	import { DEFAULT_MODEL } from '../../src/llm/config.ts';
    12	import { MAX_PLAYERS, MIN_PLAYERS } from '../../src/engine/setup.ts';
    13	
    14	describe('parseArgs — help', () => {
    15	  test.each([['--help'], ['-h']])('%s asks for usage', (flag) => {
    16	    const parsed = parseArgs([flag]);
    17	    expect(parsed.kind).toBe('help');
    18	    if (parsed.kind !== 'help') throw new Error('expected help');
    19	    expect(parsed.text).toBe(USAGE);
    20	  });
    21	
    22	  test('usage documents every flag and the launch command', () => {
    23	    expect(USAGE).toContain('bun run src/cli.ts');
    24	    for (const flag of ['--seed', '--players', '--model', '--no-llm', '--help']) {
    25	      expect(USAGE).toContain(flag);
    26	    }
    27	    // The default model must be documented from the one constant that defines
    28	    // it, so the help text cannot drift away from what the client actually uses.
    29	    expect(USAGE).toContain(DEFAULT_MODEL);
    30	    expect(USAGE).toContain(`${MIN_PLAYERS}-${MAX_PLAYERS}`);
    31	  });
    32	
    33	  test('help wins even when other flags are present', () => {
    34	    expect(parseArgs(['--players', '9', '--help']).kind).toBe('help');
    35	  });
    36	});
    37	
    38	describe('parseArgs — defaults', () => {
    39	  test('no arguments means three seats, the LLM on, and a generated seed', () => {
    40	    const parsed = parseArgs([], () => 4242);
    41	    if (parsed.kind !== 'run') throw new Error(`expected run, got ${parsed.kind}`);
    42	    expect(parsed.options).toEqual({
    43	      seed: 4242,
    44	      seedWasGiven: false,
    45	      players: MIN_PLAYERS,
    46	      useLlm: true,
    47	    });
    48	  });
    49	
    50	  test('the generated seed is what the caller supplies, so a replay is printable', () => {
    51	    const parsed = parseArgs([], () => 7);
    52	    if (parsed.kind !== 'run') throw new Error('expected run');
    53	    expect(parsed.options.seed).toBe(7);
    54	    expect(parsed.options.seedWasGiven).toBe(false);
    55	  });
    56	});
    57	
    58	describe('parseArgs — flags', () => {
    59	  test('reads seed, players, model and --no-llm together', () => {
    60	    const parsed = parseArgs(['--seed', '11', '--players', '5', '--model', 'gpt-4o', '--no-llm']);
    61	    if (parsed.kind !== 'run') throw new Error('expected run');
    62	    expect(parsed.options).toEqual({
    63	      seed: 11,
    64	      seedWasGiven: true,
    65	      players: 5,
    66	      model: 'gpt-4o',
    67	      useLlm: false,
    68	    });
    69	  });
    70	
    71	  test('--flag=value form is accepted', () => {
    72	    const parsed = parseArgs(['--seed=3', '--players=4']);
    73	    if (parsed.kind !== 'run') throw new Error('expected run');
    74	    expect(parsed.options.seed).toBe(3);
    75	    expect(parsed.options.players).toBe(4);
    76	  });
    77	
    78	  test('a negative seed is a seed, not a missing value', () => {
    79	    const parsed = parseArgs(['--seed', '-5']);
    80	    if (parsed.kind !== 'run') throw new Error('expected run');
    81	    expect(parsed.options.seed).toBe(-5);
    82	  });
    83	});
    84	
    85	describe('parseArgs — refusals', () => {
    86	  test.each([
    87	    ['2 seats', ['--players', '2'], `between ${MIN_PLAYERS} and ${MAX_PLAYERS}`],
    88	    ['7 seats', ['--players', '7'], `between ${MIN_PLAYERS} and ${MAX_PLAYERS}`],
    89	    ['fractional seats', ['--players', '3.5'], `between ${MIN_PLAYERS} and ${MAX_PLAYERS}`],
    90	    ['non-numeric seats', ['--players', 'lots'], `between ${MIN_PLAYERS} and ${MAX_PLAYERS}`],
    91	    ['non-numeric seed', ['--seed', 'abc'], '--seed'],
    92	    ['seed with no value', ['--seed'], '--seed'],
    93	    ['players with no value', ['--players'], '--players'],
    94	    ['model with no value', ['--model'], '--model'],
    95	    ['unknown flag', ['--turbo'], '--turbo'],
    96	    ['stray argument', ['play'], 'play'],
    97	  ])('%s is refused with a message naming the problem', (_name, argv, needle) => {
    98	    const parsed = parseArgs(argv);
    99	    expect(parsed.kind).toBe('error');
   100	    if (parsed.kind !== 'error') throw new Error('expected error');
   101	    expect(parsed.message).toContain(needle);
   102	    // Every refusal points at the one command that explains the flags.
   103	    expect(parsed.message).toContain('--help');
   104	  });
   105	
   106	  test('the bounds refusal names the flag it is about', () => {
   107	    const parsed = parseArgs(['--players', '2']);
   108	    if (parsed.kind !== 'error') throw new Error('expected error');
   109	    expect(parsed.message).toContain('--players');
   110	  });
   111	});

   197	  async function prompt(text: string): Promise<string | null> {
   198	    const answer = await io.ask(text);
   199	    if (answer === null) ended = true;
   200	    return answer;
   201	  }
   202	
   203	  /**
   204	   * Print a menu and read one answer. Null means "cancelled or gone".
   205	   *
   206	   * `hidden` options are matchable but not printed: the command vocabulary is
   207	   * always available to type (ADR-0003), while the numbered list shows only what
   208	   * makes sense right now. A keyword that is legal to SAY but not legal to DO
   209	   * reaches the engine and comes back as its own refusal, which is better than
   210	   * pretending the word does not exist.
   211	   */
   212	  async function choose<T>(
   213	    title: string,
   214	    choices: readonly Option<T>[],
   215	    hidden: readonly Option<T>[] = [],
   216	  ): Promise<T | null> {
   217	    while (!ended) {
   218	      io.write('');
   219	      io.write(title);
   220	      choices.forEach((entry, index) => {
   221	        io.write(`  ${index + 1}) ${entry.key.length === 0 ? '' : `${entry.key} — `}${entry.label}`);
   222	      });
   223	      const answer = await prompt('> ');
   224	      if (answer === null) return null;
   225	      const match = resolveChoice(answer, [...choices, ...hidden]);
   226	      if (match !== null) return match.value;
   227	      io.write(`I do not understand "${answer}". Answer with a number, or with one of the keywords.`);
   228	    }
   229	    return null;
   230	  }

   232	  /** Show the player everything that has happened since the last time. */
   233	  async function flush(): Promise<void> {
   234	    const fresh = state.events.slice(narrated);
   235	    narrated = state.events.length;
   236	    const mine = fresh.filter((event) => isVisibleTo(event, HUMAN_SEAT));
   237	    if (mine.length === 0) return;
   238	
   239	    const lines =
   240	      gm === null ? fallbackNarration(mine) : await gm.narrate(playerView(state, HUMAN_SEAT), mine);
   241	    if (gm !== null && !llmNoticed && lines.some((line) => line.source === 'fallback')) {
   242	      llmNoticed = true;
   243	      io.write(FALLBACK_NOTICE);
   244	    }
   245	    for (const line of lines) io.write(`  ${line.text}`);
   246	  }
   247	
   248	  function cardOptions<T extends Card>(cards: readonly T[]): Option<T | null>[] {
   249	    return [
   250	      ...cards.map((card) => option<T | null>('', String(card), card)),
   251	      option<T | null>('back', 'never mind', null),
   252	    ];
   253	  }
   254	
   255	  async function doSuggest(view: PlayerView): Promise<void> {
   256	    if (!canSuggest(state) || view.position.kind !== 'room') {
   257	      // The word was typed when the rule does not allow it. `canSuggest` is the
   258	      // predicate `makeSuggestion` itself checks, so this call is certain to
   259	      // throw — and the player gets the ENGINE's reason rather than a second,
   260	      // drifting copy of the rule kept here.
   261	      state = makeSuggestion(state, { suspect: SUSPECTS[0] as Suspect, weapon: WEAPONS[0] as Weapon });
   262	      return;
   263	    }
   264	    const room = view.position.room;
   265	    const suspect = await choose(
   266	      `Suggestion — who do you say was here in the ${room}?`,
   267	      cardOptions(SUSPECTS),
   268	    );
   269	    if (suspect === null) return;
   270	    const weapon = await choose(`Suggestion — with what, in the ${room}?`, cardOptions(WEAPONS));
   271	    if (weapon === null) return;
   272	    state = makeSuggestion(state, { suspect: suspect as Suspect, weapon: weapon as Weapon });
   273	  }
   274	
   275	  async function doAccuse(): Promise<void> {
   276	    io.write('');
   277	    io.write('An accusation is final: name it wrongly and you are out of the running.');
   278	    const suspect = await choose('Who did it?', cardOptions(SUSPECTS));
   279	    if (suspect === null) return;
   280	    const weapon = await choose('With what?', cardOptions(WEAPONS));
   281	    if (weapon === null) return;
   282	    const room = await choose('Where?', cardOptions(ROOMS));
   283	    if (room === null) return;
   284	    state = makeAccusation(state, {
   285	      suspect: suspect as Suspect,
   286	      weapon: weapon as Weapon,
   287	      room: room as Room,
   288	    });
   289	  }
   290	
   291	  async function doAsk(view: PlayerView): Promise<void> {
   292	    if (gm === null) throw new IllegalActionError('there is no game master to ask');
   293	    const others = SUSPECTS.filter((suspect) => suspect !== view.character);
   294	    const suspect = await choose('Whom will you question?', cardOptions(others));
   295	    if (suspect === null) return;
   296	    const question = await prompt(`Ask ${suspect}: `);
   297	    if (question === null || question.length === 0) return;
   298	    askedOnTurn = view.turnNumber;
   299	    const answer = await gm.ask(view, suspect as Suspect, question);
   300	    if (!llmNoticed && answer.source === 'fallback') {
   301	      llmNoticed = true;
   302	      io.write(FALLBACK_NOTICE);
   303	    }
   304	    io.write('');
   305	    io.write(`  ${answer.suspect}: "${answer.text}"`);
   306	  }
   307	
   308	  /** The menu for the player's current phase, in the order a player wants it. */
   309	  function actionsFor(view: PlayerView): Option<Action>[] {
   310	    const choices: Option<Action>[] = [];
   311	
   312	    if (view.eliminated) {
   313	      choices.push(option('end', 'end your turn', { kind: 'end' } as const));
   314	    } else if (state.phase === 'awaiting-move') {
   315	      const moves = legalMoves(state);
   316	      for (const move of moves) {
   317	        choices.push(option('', `move to ${describeDestination(move)}`, {
   318	          kind: 'move',
   319	          position: move.position,
   320	        } as const));
   321	      }
   322	      if (moves.length === 0) {
   323	        choices.push(option('end', 'nowhere to go — end your turn', { kind: 'end' } as const));
   324	      }
   325	    } else {
   326	      if (canTakeSecretPassage(state) && view.position.kind === 'room') {
   327	        const through = secretPassageFrom(view.position.room);
   328	        if (through !== null) {
   329	          choices.push(option('passage', `take the secret passage to the ${through}`, {
   330	            kind: 'passage',
   331	          } as const));
   332	        }
   333	      }
   334	      if (state.phase === 'awaiting-roll') {
   335	        choices.push(option('roll', 'roll the die and move', { kind: 'roll' } as const));
   336	      }
   337	      if (canSuggest(state)) {
   338	        choices.push(option('suggest', 'make a suggestion', { kind: 'suggest' } as const));
   339	      }
   340	      if (state.phase === 'awaiting-action') {
   341	        choices.push(option('end', 'end your turn', { kind: 'end' } as const));
   342	      }
   343	    }
   344	
   345	    if (!view.eliminated) {
   346	      choices.push(option('accuse', 'make an accusation', { kind: 'accuse' } as const));
   347	      if (gm !== null && askedOnTurn !== view.turnNumber) {
   348	        choices.push(option('ask', 'question a suspect', { kind: 'ask' } as const));
   349	      }
   350	    }
   351	    choices.push(option('notes', 'read your notebook', { kind: 'notes' } as const));
   352	    choices.push(option('quit', 'leave the game', { kind: 'quit' } as const));
   353	    return choices;
   354	  }
   355	
   356	  async function humanAction(): Promise<void> {
   357	    const view = playerView(state, HUMAN_SEAT);
   358	    if (view.turnNumber !== shownTurn) {
   359	      shownTurn = view.turnNumber;
   360	      for (const line of renderStatus(view)) io.write(line);
   361	    }
   362	
   363	    const printed = actionsFor(view);
   364	    const action = await choose('What will you do?', printed, unlistedVocabulary(printed));
   365	    if (action === null) return;
   366	
   367	    try {
   368	      switch (action.kind) {
   369	        case 'roll':
   370	          state = rollDice(state);
   371	          return;
   372	        case 'passage':
   373	          state = takeSecretPassage(state);
   374	          return;
   375	        case 'move':
   376	          state = moveTo(state, action.position);
   377	          return;
   378	        case 'suggest':
   379	          await doSuggest(view);
   380	          return;
   381	        case 'accuse':
   382	          await doAccuse();
   383	          return;
   384	        case 'ask':
   385	          await doAsk(view);
   386	          return;
   387	        case 'notes':
   388	          for (const line of renderNotebook(view, buildNotebook(view))) io.write(line);
   389	          io.write('  Where everyone stands:');
   390	          for (const line of renderSuspects(view)) io.write(line);

   395	        case 'quit':
   396	          ended = true;
   397	          return;

   399	    } catch (error) {
   400	      // A menu offered something the engine will not allow: say so plainly and
   401	      // let the player choose again. The game never dies of a bad choice.
   402	      if (!(error instanceof IllegalActionError)) throw error;
   403	      io.write('');
   404	      io.write(`That will not work: ${error.message}`);
   405	    }

   407	
   408	  /** The player owes a refutation: which of their matching cards do they show? */
   409	  async function humanRefutation(): Promise<void> {
   410	    const view = playerView(state, HUMAN_SEAT);
   411	    const pending = view.pendingRefutation;
   412	    if (pending === null || !pending.yours || pending.options === null) return;
   413	
   414	    const card = await choose(
   415	      `${pending.suggester} is waiting — you must show ONE of these, privately:`,
   416	      pending.options.map((candidate) => option('', String(candidate), candidate)),
   417	    );
   418	    if (card === null) return;
   419	    try {
   420	      state = provideRefutationCard(state, card);
   421	    } catch (error) {
   422	      if (!(error instanceof IllegalActionError)) throw error;
   423	      io.write(`That will not work: ${error.message}`);
   424	    }
   425	  }
   426	
   427	  // Everything that has happened is told BEFORE the next question is asked, so
   428	  // the player never chooses without having seen the board move.
   429	  while (!state.over && !ended) {
   430	    await flush();
   431	    if (state.phase === 'awaiting-refutation') {
   432	      if (state.pendingRefutation?.refuter === HUMAN_SEAT) await humanRefutation();
   433	      else state = refuteAsOpponent(state);
   434	      continue;
   435	    }
   436	    if (currentPlayer(state).id === HUMAN_SEAT) {
   437	      await humanAction();
   438	      continue;
   439	    }
   440	    state = playOpponentTurn(state);
   441	  }
   442	  await flush();
   443	
   444	  const view = playerView(state, HUMAN_SEAT);
   445	  if (state.over) {
   446	    for (const line of renderOutcome(view)) io.write(line);
   447	  } else {
   448	    io.write('');
   449	    io.write('You leave the house with the case unsolved.');
   450	  }
   451	
   452	  io.write('');
   453	  for (const line of formatUsageLedger(client === null ? NO_USAGE : client.usage)) io.write(line);
   454	  return 0;
   455	}
     1	/**
     2	 * Text rendering for the terminal UI.
     3	 *
     4	 * Every function here is pure — a view in, lines out — and every one of them
     5	 * reads a `PlayerView` or a `Notebook`, never a `GameState`. That is not a
     6	 * style preference: `GameState` carries the case file and every hand, and the
     7	 * screen is the one place both would be spilled (ADR-0001, view.ts).
     8	 */
     9	
    10	import type { Destination } from '../engine/board.ts';
    11	import { SUSPECTS, WEAPONS, type Card } from '../engine/cards.ts';
    12	import type { GameEvent } from '../engine/types.ts';
    13	import { describePosition, type PlayerView } from '../engine/view.ts';
    14	import type { Notebook } from '../gm/notebook.ts';
    15	import type { Scenario } from '../gm/scenario.ts';
    16	
    17	/** A card list in a stable, readable order. */
    18	function list(cards: readonly Card[]): string {
    19	  return cards.length === 0 ? '(none)' : [...cards].join(', ');
    20	}
    21	
    22	/** The opening: seed for replay, the roster, and the scenario's own paragraph. */
    23	export function renderOpening(
    24	  scenario: Scenario,
    25	  session: { readonly seed: number; readonly players: number; readonly llm: string },
    26	): string[] {
    27	  return [
    28	    '',
    29	    `${scenario.setting}`,
    30	    `The dead: ${scenario.victim}`,
    31	    '',
    32	    scenario.intro,
    33	    '',
    34	    `Seed ${session.seed} · ${session.players} at the table · ${session.llm}`,
    35	    `Replay this exact game with: bun run src/cli.ts --seed ${session.seed} --players ${session.players}`,
    36	  ];
    37	}
    38	
    39	/** The header for one of the player's turns: where they are and what they hold. */
    40	export function renderStatus(view: PlayerView): string[] {
    41	  const seats = view.opponents.map(
    42	    (opponent) =>
    43	      `${opponent.id} (${opponent.character}) in ${describePosition(opponent.position)}, ` +
    44	      `${opponent.handSize} ${opponent.handSize === 1 ? 'card' : 'cards'}` +
    45	      `${opponent.eliminated ? ', out of the running' : ''}`,
    46	  );
    47	  return [
    48	    '',
    49	    `── Turn ${view.turnNumber} — you are ${view.character}, in ${describePosition(view.position)} ──`,
    50	    `Your hand: ${list(view.hand)}`,
    51	    ...seats.map((seat) => `  ${seat}`),
    52	    ...(view.eliminated ? ['You accused wrongly: you may still refute, but you cannot win.'] : []),
    53	  ];
    54	}
    55	
    56	/** One movement option as a phrase: where it goes and what it costs. */
    57	export function describeDestination(destination: Destination): string {
    58	  return `${describePosition(destination.position)} (${destination.steps} ${
    59	    destination.steps === 1 ? 'step' : 'steps'
    60	  })`;
    61	}
    62	
    63	/**
    64	 * The deduction notebook: what this seat has PROVEN and what is still open.
    65	 * Everything shown is derived from the seat's own view, so it gives away
    66	 * nothing the player was not already entitled to work out.
    67	 */
    68	export function renderNotebook(view: PlayerView, notebook: Notebook): string[] {
    69	  const elsewhere = [...notebook.held]
    70	    .filter(([, holder]) => holder !== view.you)
    71	    .map(([card, holder]) => `${card} (${holder})`);
    72	  const proven = [...notebook.caseFileCards];
    73	
    74	  return [
    75	    '',
    76	    `Notebook — turn ${view.turnNumber}`,
    77	    `  Your hand: ${list(view.hand)}`,
    78	    `  Proven in another hand: ${elsewhere.length === 0 ? '(nothing yet)' : elsewhere.join(', ')}`,
    79	    ...(proven.length === 0 ? [] : [`  Proven in the case file: ${proven.join(', ')}`]),
    80	    `  Suspects still possible: ${list(notebook.suspects)}`,
    81	    `  Weapons still possible: ${list(notebook.weapons)}`,
    82	    `  Rooms still possible: ${list(notebook.rooms)}`,
    83	    `  Weapons on the board: ${WEAPONS.map((weapon) => `${weapon} — ${view.weaponPositions[weapon]}`).join('; ')}`,
    84	    ...(notebook.solution === null
    85	      ? []
    86	      : [
    87	          `  Only one answer is left: ${notebook.solution.suspect} in the ${notebook.solution.room} ` +
    88	            `with the ${notebook.solution.weapon}.`,
    89	        ]),
    90	  ];
    91	}
    92	
    93	/** Where every suspect token stands — public, and what a suggestion moves. */
    94	export function renderSuspects(view: PlayerView): string[] {
    95	  return SUSPECTS.map((suspect) => `  ${suspect} — ${describePosition(view.suspectPositions[suspect])}`);
    96	}
    97	
    98	/** The case file from the game-over event, or null before the game ends. */
    99	function finalEvent(view: PlayerView): Extract<GameEvent, { type: 'game-over' }> | null {
   100	  for (let index = view.events.length - 1; index >= 0; index -= 1) {
   101	    const event = view.events[index];
   102	    if (event?.type === 'game-over') return event;
   103	  }
   104	  return null;
   105	}
   106	
   107	/** The end of the game: the answer, and where the player finished. */
   108	export function renderOutcome(view: PlayerView): string[] {
   109	  const ending = finalEvent(view);
   110	  const lines = ['', '── Case closed ──'];
   111	  if (ending !== null) {
   112	    lines.push(
   113	      `The answer: ${ending.caseFile.suspect} in the ${ending.caseFile.room} with the ${ending.caseFile.weapon}.`,
   114	    );
   115	  }
   116	  if (view.winner === view.you) lines.push('You solved it. The house is yours.');
   117	  else if (view.winner === null) lines.push('Nobody solved it. The case stays open.');
   118	  else lines.push(`${view.winner} got there first. You lose this one.`);
   119	  return lines;
   120	}

+/**
+ * The terminal game loop (ADR-0003).
+ *
+ * The loop is a reader and a printer. Every outcome it shows was decided by the
+ * engine, every legality question it asks is answered by the engine, and the
+ * game master is asked only for words. The three consequences that shape this
+ * file:
+ *
+ *  - It takes an `Io` and a client FACTORY rather than reaching for stdio or the
+ *    network, so a test drives the real loop over the real engine with scripted
+ *    answers, and `--no-llm` is a code path that provably makes no call at all.
+ *  - Every engine call is wrapped: an `IllegalActionError` is a menu that was
+ *    offered when it should not have been, and the player gets the menu back
+ *    rather than a stack trace.
+ *  - The player is `p1`, seat 1. Everything the loop renders comes from that
+ *    seat's `playerView` — the loop never touches `GameState.caseFile` or
+ *    another seat's hand, so the screen cannot spill them.
+ *
+ * The session always ends with the token ledger, measured from each response's
+ * own `usage` object (PRODUCT.md hard constraint 6).
+ */
+
+import type { Position } from '../engine/board.ts';
+import { SUSPECTS, WEAPONS, ROOMS, type Card, type Suspect, type Weapon, type Room } from '../engine/cards.ts';
+import { secretPassageFrom } from '../engine/board.ts';
+import {
+  canSuggest,
+  canTakeSecretPassage,
+  currentPlayer,
+  endTurn,
+  legalMoves,
+  makeAccusation,
+  makeSuggestion,
+  moveTo,
+  provideRefutationCard,
+  rollDice,
+  takeSecretPassage,
+} from '../engine/actions.ts';
+import { createGame } from '../engine/setup.ts';
+import { IllegalActionError, type GameState } from '../engine/types.ts';
+import { isVisibleTo, playerView, type PlayerView } from '../engine/view.ts';
+import {
+  CANNED_SCENARIO,
+  GameMaster,
+  buildNotebook,
+  fallbackNarration,
+  formatUsageLedger,
+  playOpponentTurn,
+  refuteAsOpponent,
+} from '../gm/index.ts';
+import type { ChatClient, SessionUsage } from '../llm/index.ts';
+import type { Io } from './io.ts';
+import {
+  describeDestination,
+  renderNotebook,
+  renderOpening,
+  renderOutcome,
+  renderStatus,
+  renderSuspects,
+} from './render.ts';
+
+/** Seat 1 is the human. The rest are played by the deterministic policy. */
+export const HUMAN_SEAT = 'p1';
+
+export type LoopOptions = {
+  readonly seed: number | string;
+  readonly players: number;
+  /** False under `--no-llm`: no client is built and no request is made. */
+  readonly useLlm: boolean;
+};
+
+export type LoopDeps = {
+  readonly io: Io;
+  /**
+   * Builds the session's one chat client. Called only when `useLlm`, and a
+   * throw (a missing key, most often) is a notice and an offline game, not a
+   * crash — the game must be playable without a model (hard constraint 4).
+   */
+  readonly createClient?: () => ChatClient;
+};
+
+/** The ledger for a session that never built a client. */
+const NO_USAGE: SessionUsage = {
+  calls: 0,
+  callsWithUsage: 0,
+  callsWithoutUsage: 0,
+  prompt_tokens: 0,
+  completion_tokens: 0,
+  total_tokens: 0,
+  byModel: {},
+};
+
+const FALLBACK_NOTICE =
+  '(the game master is not answering — the rest of this game is told in the engine\'s own words)';
+
+type Option<T> = { readonly key: string; readonly label: string; readonly value: T };
+
+type Action =
+  | { readonly kind: 'roll' }
+  | { readonly kind: 'passage' }
+  | { readonly kind: 'move'; readonly position: Position }
+  | { readonly kind: 'suggest' }
+  | { readonly kind: 'accuse' }
+  | { readonly kind: 'ask' }
+  | { readonly kind: 'notes' }
+  | { readonly kind: 'end' }
+  | { readonly kind: 'quit' };
+
+function option<T>(key: string, label: string, value: T): Option<T> {
+  return { key, label, value };
+}
+
+/**
+ * The whole command vocabulary (ADR-0003), independent of what is legal now.
+ * A word from this list is always understood; whether it can be DONE is the
+ * engine's call, and its refusal is what the player is shown.
+ */
+const VOCABULARY: readonly Option<Action>[] = [
+  option('roll', 'roll the die and move', { kind: 'roll' }),
+  option('passage', 'take the secret passage', { kind: 'passage' }),
+  option('suggest', 'make a suggestion', { kind: 'suggest' }),
+  option('accuse', 'make an accusation', { kind: 'accuse' }),
+  option('ask', 'question a suspect', { kind: 'ask' }),
+  option('notes', 'read your notebook', { kind: 'notes' }),
+  option('end', 'end your turn', { kind: 'end' }),
+  option('quit', 'leave the game', { kind: 'quit' }),
+];
+
+/** The vocabulary this menu does not already show. */
+function unlistedVocabulary(printed: readonly Option<Action>[]): Option<Action>[] {
+  return VOCABULARY.filter((word) => !printed.some((entry) => entry.key === word.key));
+}
+
+/**
+ * Resolve one typed answer against a menu: a number picks by position, a word
+ * picks by keyword (exactly, or by an unambiguous prefix). Returns null when
+ * the answer matches nothing, which is a reprint rather than a failure.
+ */
+export function resolveChoice<T>(answer: string, options: readonly Option<T>[]): Option<T> | null {
+  const tidy = answer.trim().toLowerCase();
+  if (tidy.length === 0) return null;
+
+  if (/^\d+$/.test(tidy)) {
+    const index = Number(tidy) - 1;
+    return options[index] ?? null;
+  }
+
+  const exact = options.filter((candidate) => candidate.key.toLowerCase() === tidy);
+  if (exact.length === 1) return exact[0] as Option<T>;
+
+  const byPrefix = options.filter(
+    (candidate) => candidate.key.length > 0 && candidate.key.toLowerCase().startsWith(tidy),
+  );
+  return byPrefix.length === 1 ? (byPrefix[0] as Option<T>) : null;
+}
+
+/**
+ * Play one session to its end.
+ *
+ * Returns the process exit code: 0 for a game that ended or a player who left.
+ */
+export async function runGame(options: LoopOptions, deps: LoopDeps): Promise<number> {
+  const { io } = deps;
+
+  let client: ChatClient | null = null;
+  if (options.useLlm && deps.createClient !== undefined) {
+    try {
+      client = deps.createClient();
+    } catch (error) {
+      io.write(`(no game master: ${error instanceof Error ? error.message : String(error)})`);
+      io.write('(playing offline — the game is the same, the prose is plainer)');
+    }
+  }
+  const gm = client === null ? null : new GameMaster(client);
+
+  let state: GameState = createGame({ seed: options.seed, playerCount: options.players });
+  let narrated = 0;
+  let shownTurn = -1;
+  let askedOnTurn = -1;
+  let llmNoticed = false;
+  /** Set when stdin closes or the player quits: every menu unwinds. */
+  let ended = false;
+
+  const scenario = gm === null ? CANNED_SCENARIO : await gm.openScenario();
+  for (const line of renderOpening(scenario, {
+    seed: typeof options.seed === 'number' ? options.seed : Number(options.seed),
+    players: options.players,
+    llm: client === null ? 'offline (--no-llm)' : `game master: ${client.config.model}`,
+  })) {
+    io.write(line);
+  }
+  if (gm !== null && scenario.source === 'fallback') {
+    llmNoticed = true;
+    io.write(FALLBACK_NOTICE);
+  }
+
+  async function prompt(text: string): Promise<string | null> {
+    const answer = await io.ask(text);
+    if (answer === null) ended = true;
+    return answer;
+  }
+
+  /**
+   * Print a menu and read one answer. Null means "cancelled or gone".
+   *
+   * `hidden` options are matchable but not printed: the command vocabulary is
+   * always available to type (ADR-0003), while the numbered list shows only what
+   * makes sense right now. A keyword that is legal to SAY but not legal to DO
+   * reaches the engine and comes back as its own refusal, which is better than
+   * pretending the word does not exist.
+   */
+  async function choose<T>(
+    title: string,
+    choices: readonly Option<T>[],
+    hidden: readonly Option<T>[] = [],
+  ): Promise<T | null> {
+    while (!ended) {
+      io.write('');
+      io.write(title);
+      choices.forEach((entry, index) => {
+        io.write(`  ${index + 1}) ${entry.key.length === 0 ? '' : `${entry.key} — `}${entry.label}`);
+      });
+      const answer = await prompt('> ');
+      if (answer === null) return null;
+      const match = resolveChoice(answer, [...choices, ...hidden]);
+      if (match !== null) return match.value;
+      io.write(`I do not understand "${answer}". Answer with a number, or with one of the keywords.`);
+    }
+    return null;
+  }
+
+  /** Show the player everything that has happened since the last time. */
+  async function flush(): Promise<void> {
+    const fresh = state.events.slice(narrated);
+    narrated = state.events.length;
+    const mine = fresh.filter((event) => isVisibleTo(event, HUMAN_SEAT));
+    if (mine.length === 0) return;
+
+    const lines =
+      gm === null ? fallbackNarration(mine) : await gm.narrate(playerView(state, HUMAN_SEAT), mine);
+    if (gm !== null && !llmNoticed && lines.some((line) => line.source === 'fallback')) {
+      llmNoticed = true;
+      io.write(FALLBACK_NOTICE);
+    }
+    for (const line of lines) io.write(`  ${line.text}`);
+  }
+
+  function cardOptions<T extends Card>(cards: readonly T[]): Option<T | null>[] {
+    return [
+      ...cards.map((card) => option<T | null>('', String(card), card)),
+      option<T | null>('back', 'never mind', null),
+    ];
+  }
+
+  async function doSuggest(view: PlayerView): Promise<void> {
+    if (!canSuggest(state) || view.position.kind !== 'room') {
+      // The word was typed when the rule does not allow it. `canSuggest` is the
+      // predicate `makeSuggestion` itself checks, so this call is certain to
+      // throw — and the player gets the ENGINE's reason rather than a second,
+      // drifting copy of the rule kept here.
+      state = makeSuggestion(state, { suspect: SUSPECTS[0] as Suspect, weapon: WEAPONS[0] as Weapon });
+      return;
+    }
+    const room = view.position.room;
+    const suspect = await choose(
+      `Suggestion — who do you say was here in the ${room}?`,
+      cardOptions(SUSPECTS),
+    );
+    if (suspect === null) return;
+    const weapon = await choose(`Suggestion — with what, in the ${room}?`, cardOptions(WEAPONS));
+    if (weapon === null) return;
+    state = makeSuggestion(state, { suspect: suspect as Suspect, weapon: weapon as Weapon });
+  }
+
+  async function doAccuse(): Promise<void> {
+    io.write('');
+    io.write('An accusation is final: name it wrongly and you are out of the running.');
+    const suspect = await choose('Who did it?', cardOptions(SUSPECTS));
+    if (suspect === null) return;
+    const weapon = await choose('With what?', cardOptions(WEAPONS));
+    if (weapon === null) return;
+    const room = await choose('Where?', cardOptions(ROOMS));
+    if (room === null) return;
+    state = makeAccusation(state, {
+      suspect: suspect as Suspect,
+      weapon: weapon as Weapon,
+      room: room as Room,
+    });
+  }
+
+  async function doAsk(view: PlayerView): Promise<void> {
+    if (gm === null) throw new IllegalActionError('there is no game master to ask');
+    const others = SUSPECTS.filter((suspect) => suspect !== view.character);
+    const suspect = await choose('Whom will you question?', cardOptions(others));
+    if (suspect === null) return;
+    const question = await prompt(`Ask ${suspect}: `);
+    if (question === null || question.length === 0) return;
+    askedOnTurn = view.turnNumber;
+    const answer = await gm.ask(view, suspect as Suspect, question);
+    if (!llmNoticed && answer.source === 'fallback') {
+      llmNoticed = true;
+      io.write(FALLBACK_NOTICE);
+    }
+    io.write('');
+    io.write(`  ${answer.suspect}: "${answer.text}"`);
+  }
+
+  /** The menu for the player's current phase, in the order a player wants it. */
+  function actionsFor(view: PlayerView): Option<Action>[] {
+    const choices: Option<Action>[] = [];
+
+    if (view.eliminated) {
+      choices.push(option('end', 'end your turn', { kind: 'end' } as const));
+    } else if (state.phase === 'awaiting-move') {
+      const moves = legalMoves(state);
+      for (const move of moves) {
+        choices.push(option('', `move to ${describeDestination(move)}`, {
+          kind: 'move',
+          position: move.position,
+        } as const));
+      }
+      if (moves.length === 0) {
+        choices.push(option('end', 'nowhere to go — end your turn', { kind: 'end' } as const));
+      }
+    } else {
+      if (canTakeSecretPassage(state) && view.position.kind === 'room') {
+        const through = secretPassageFrom(view.position.room);
+        if (through !== null) {
+          choices.push(option('passage', `take the secret passage to the ${through}`, {
+            kind: 'passage',
+          } as const));
+        }
+      }
+      if (state.phase === 'awaiting-roll') {
+        choices.push(option('roll', 'roll the die and move', { kind: 'roll' } as const));
+      }
+      if (canSuggest(state)) {
+        choices.push(option('suggest', 'make a suggestion', { kind: 'suggest' } as const));
+      }
+      if (state.phase === 'awaiting-action') {
+        choices.push(option('end', 'end your turn', { kind: 'end' } as const));
+      }
+    }
+
+    if (!view.eliminated) {
+      choices.push(option('accuse', 'make an accusation', { kind: 'accuse' } as const));
+      if (gm !== null && askedOnTurn !== view.turnNumber) {
+        choices.push(option('ask', 'question a suspect', { kind: 'ask' } as const));
+      }
+    }
+    choices.push(option('notes', 'read your notebook', { kind: 'notes' } as const));
+    choices.push(option('quit', 'leave the game', { kind: 'quit' } as const));
+    return choices;
+  }
+
+  async function humanAction(): Promise<void> {
+    const view = playerView(state, HUMAN_SEAT);
+    if (view.turnNumber !== shownTurn) {
+      shownTurn = view.turnNumber;
+      for (const line of renderStatus(view)) io.write(line);
+    }
+
+    const printed = actionsFor(view);
+    const action = await choose('What will you do?', printed, unlistedVocabulary(printed));
+    if (action === null) return;
+
+    try {
+      switch (action.kind) {
+        case 'roll':
+          state = rollDice(state);
+          return;
+        case 'passage':
+          state = takeSecretPassage(state);
+          return;
+        case 'move':
+          state = moveTo(state, action.position);
+          return;
+        case 'suggest':
+          await doSuggest(view);
+          return;
+        case 'accuse':
+          await doAccuse();
+          return;
+        case 'ask':
+          await doAsk(view);
+          return;
+        case 'notes':
+          for (const line of renderNotebook(view, buildNotebook(view))) io.write(line);
+          io.write('  Where everyone stands:');
+          for (const line of renderSuspects(view)) io.write(line);
+          return;
+        case 'end':
+          state = endTurn(state);
+          return;
+        case 'quit':
+          ended = true;
+          return;
+      }
+    } catch (error) {
+      // A menu offered something the engine will not allow: say so plainly and
+      // let the player choose again. The game never dies of a bad choice.
+      if (!(error instanceof IllegalActionError)) throw error;
+      io.write('');
+      io.write(`That will not work: ${error.message}`);
+    }
+  }
+
+  /** The player owes a refutation: which of their matching cards do they show? */
+  async function humanRefutation(): Promise<void> {
+    const view = playerView(state, HUMAN_SEAT);
+    const pending = view.pendingRefutation;
+    if (pending === null || !pending.yours || pending.options === null) return;
+
+    const card = await choose(
+      `${pending.suggester} is waiting — you must show ONE of these, privately:`,
+      pending.options.map((candidate) => option('', String(candidate), candidate)),
+    );
+    if (card === null) return;
+    try {
+      state = provideRefutationCard(state, card);
+    } catch (error) {
+      if (!(error instanceof IllegalActionError)) throw error;
+      io.write(`That will not work: ${error.message}`);
+    }
+  }
+
+  // Everything that has happened is told BEFORE the next question is asked, so
+  // the player never chooses without having seen the board move.
+  while (!state.over && !ended) {
+    await flush();
+    if (state.phase === 'awaiting-refutation') {
+      if (state.pendingRefutation?.refuter === HUMAN_SEAT) await humanRefutation();
+      else state = refuteAsOpponent(state);
+      continue;
+    }
+    if (currentPlayer(state).id === HUMAN_SEAT) {
+      await humanAction();
+      continue;
+    }
+    state = playOpponentTurn(state);
+  }
+  await flush();
+
+  const view = playerView(state, HUMAN_SEAT);
+  if (state.over) {
+    for (const line of renderOutcome(view)) io.write(line);
+  } else {
+    io.write('');
+    io.write('You leave the house with the case unsolved.');
+  }
+
+  io.write('');
+  for (const line of formatUsageLedger(client === null ? NO_USAGE : client.usage)) io.write(line);
+  return 0;
+}
