     1	/**
     2	 * Projections of game state for consumers that must NOT see everything.
     3	 *
     4	 * The CLI and the LLM game master are both untrusted with the case file and
     5	 * with other players' hands (ADR-0001: leaking either into a prompt would spoil
     6	 * the puzzle). They read a `PlayerView`, never `GameState`.
     7	 *
     8	 * `describeEvent` is also the narration fallback: when the game master returns
     9	 * nothing or errors, the engine's own plain sentence is shown instead.
    10	 */
    11	
    12	import type { Card, Room, Suspect, Weapon } from './cards.ts';
    13	import type { Position } from './board.ts';
    14	import type { GameEvent, GameState, PlayerId, TurnPhase } from './types.ts';
    15	import { IllegalActionError } from './types.ts';
    16	
    17	/**
    18	 * Whether one event is addressed to a given player — the whole visibility rule,
    19	 * in one place. Exported so a consumer holding loose events (the narrator, which
    20	 * is handed a turn's worth) applies the SAME rule the log projection does,
    21	 * rather than trusting whoever passed them in.
    22	 */
    23	export function isVisibleTo(event: GameEvent, playerId: PlayerId): boolean {
    24	  return event.visibleTo === 'all' || event.visibleTo.includes(playerId);
    25	}
    26	
    27	/** Events a given player is entitled to see, in order. */
    28	export function visibleEvents(state: GameState, playerId: PlayerId): GameEvent[] {
    29	  return state.events.filter((event) => isVisibleTo(event, playerId));
    30	}
    31	
    32	/** Events every player can see. Safe to show to a spectator or a narrator. */
    33	export function publicEvents(state: GameState): GameEvent[] {
    34	  return state.events.filter((event) => event.visibleTo === 'all');
    35	}
    36	
    37	/** The cards a player holds. Throws for an unknown player. */
    38	export function handOf(state: GameState, playerId: PlayerId): readonly Card[] {
    39	  const player = state.players.find((candidate) => candidate.id === playerId);
    40	  if (!player) throw new IllegalActionError(`no such player: ${playerId}`);
    41	  return player.hand;
    42	}
    43	
    44	export type PlayerView = {
    45	  readonly you: PlayerId;
    46	  readonly character: Suspect;
    47	  readonly hand: readonly Card[];
    48	  readonly position: Position;
    49	  readonly eliminated: boolean;
    50	  readonly phase: TurnPhase;
    51	  readonly roll: number | null;
    52	  readonly currentPlayer: PlayerId;
    53	  readonly yourTurn: boolean;
    54	  readonly turnNumber: number;
    55	  readonly over: boolean;
    56	  readonly winner: PlayerId | null;
    57	  /** Public facts about the other seats: no hands, only hand SIZES. */
    58	  readonly opponents: readonly {
    59	    readonly id: PlayerId;
    60	    readonly character: Suspect;
    61	    readonly handSize: number;
    62	    readonly eliminated: boolean;
    63	    readonly position: Position;
    64	  }[];
    65	  readonly suspectPositions: Readonly<Record<Suspect, Position>>;
    66	  readonly weaponPositions: Readonly<Record<Weapon, Room>>;
    67	  readonly events: readonly GameEvent[];
    68	  /**
    69	   * The refutation the game is waiting on, or null. Everyone may know WHO owes
    70	   * the choice — it is why play has stopped — but the cards on offer are part
    71	   * of the refuter's hand, so `options` is filled in for that player alone.
    72	   */
    73	  readonly pendingRefutation: {
    74	    readonly suggester: PlayerId;
    75	    readonly refuter: PlayerId;
    76	    readonly yours: boolean;
    77	    readonly options: readonly Card[] | null;
    78	  } | null;
    79	};
    80	
    81	/**
    82	 * Everything one player may know: their own hand, public board state, and the
    83	 * events addressed to them. The case file is absent by construction, and so is
    84	 * every other hand.
    85	 */
    86	export function playerView(state: GameState, playerId: PlayerId): PlayerView {
    87	  const player = state.players.find((candidate) => candidate.id === playerId);
    88	  if (!player) throw new IllegalActionError(`no such player: ${playerId}`);
    89	  const current = state.players[state.currentPlayerIndex];
    90	  return {
    91	    you: player.id,
    92	    character: player.character,
    93	    hand: player.hand,
    94	    position: state.suspectPositions[player.character],
    95	    eliminated: player.eliminated,
    96	    phase: state.phase,
    97	    roll: state.roll,
    98	    currentPlayer: current?.id ?? player.id,
    99	    yourTurn: current?.id === player.id,
   100	    turnNumber: state.turnNumber,
   101	    over: state.over,
   102	    winner: state.winner,
   103	    opponents: state.players
   104	      .filter((candidate) => candidate.id !== playerId)
   105	      .map((candidate) => ({
   106	        id: candidate.id,
   107	        character: candidate.character,
   108	        handSize: candidate.hand.length,
   109	        eliminated: candidate.eliminated,
   110	        position: state.suspectPositions[candidate.character],
   111	      })),
   112	    suspectPositions: state.suspectPositions,
   113	    weaponPositions: state.weaponPositions,
   114	    events: visibleEvents(state, playerId),
   115	    pendingRefutation:
   116	      state.pendingRefutation === null
   117	        ? null
   118	        : {
   119	            suggester: state.pendingRefutation.suggester,
   120	            refuter: state.pendingRefutation.refuter,
   121	            yours: state.pendingRefutation.refuter === playerId,
   122	            options:
   123	              state.pendingRefutation.refuter === playerId ? state.pendingRefutation.options : null,
   124	          },
   125	  };
   126	}
   127	
   128	/** A position as a short human phrase, e.g. "the Library" or "corridor 6,7". */
   129	export function describePosition(position: Position): string {
   130	  return position.kind === 'room' ? `the ${position.room}` : `corridor ${position.x},${position.y}`;
   131	}
   132	
   133	/** One event as a plain English sentence — the narration fallback. */
   134	export function describeEvent(event: GameEvent): string {
   135	  switch (event.type) {
   136	    case 'game-started':
   137	      return `The game begins with ${event.players
   138	        .map((entry) => `${entry.id} as ${entry.character}`)
   139	        .join(', ')}.`;
   140	    case 'turn-started':
   141	      return `Turn ${event.turn}: it is ${event.player}'s move.`;
   142	    case 'rolled':
   143	      return `${event.player} rolls a ${event.value}.`;
   144	    case 'moved':
   145	      return `${event.player} moves ${event.steps} ${
   146	        event.steps === 1 ? 'step' : 'steps'
   147	      } from ${describePosition(event.from)} to ${describePosition(event.to)}.`;
   148	    case 'secret-passage':
   149	      return `${event.player} slips through the secret passage from the ${event.from} to the ${event.to}.`;
   150	    case 'suggestion-made':
   151	      return `${event.player} suggests ${event.suspect} in the ${event.room} with the ${event.weapon}.`;
   152	    case 'token-relocated':
   153	      return `${event.token} is moved to the ${event.to}.`;
   154	    case 'suggestion-refuted':
   155	      return `${event.refuter} refutes ${event.player}'s suggestion with a card shown in private.`;
   156	    case 'refutation-card-shown':
   157	      return `${event.refuter} shows you the ${event.card}.`;
   158	    case 'suggestion-unrefuted':
   159	      return `No one can refute ${event.player}'s suggestion.`;
   160	    case 'accusation-made':
   161	      return `${event.player} accuses ${event.suspect} in the ${event.room} with the ${event.weapon} — ${
   162	        event.correct ? 'and is right' : 'and is wrong'
   163	      }.`;
   164	    case 'player-eliminated':
   165	      return `${event.player} is out of the running, but must still refute suggestions.`;
   166	    case 'turn-ended':
   167	      return `${event.player}'s turn ends.`;
   168	    case 'game-over':
   169	      return event.winner === null
   170	        ? `The game ends unsolved. It was ${event.caseFile.suspect} in the ${event.caseFile.room} with the ${event.caseFile.weapon}.`
   171	        : `${event.winner} wins: ${event.caseFile.suspect} in the ${event.caseFile.room} with the ${event.caseFile.weapon}.`;
   172	  }
   173	}
   174	
   175	/** A player's whole visible history as plain sentences. */
   176	export function describeLog(state: GameState, playerId: PlayerId): string[] {
   177	  return visibleEvents(state, playerId).map(describeEvent);
   178	}
   179	
   180	/** Every card whose location a player has proven, and where it is. */
   181	export function knownCards(state: GameState, playerId: PlayerId): Map<Card, PlayerId> {
   182	  const known = new Map<Card, PlayerId>();
   183	  for (const card of handOf(state, playerId)) known.set(card, playerId);
   184	  for (const event of visibleEvents(state, playerId)) {
   185	    if (event.type === 'refutation-card-shown') known.set(event.card, event.refuter);
   186	  }
   187	  return known;
   188	}
   189	
     1	/**
     2	 * The opponent policy: the "simple game master" that plays the suspects the
     3	 * human is not playing.
     4	 *
     5	 * Legality is entirely deterministic and entirely view-driven. `planOpponentAction`
     6	 * reads a `PlayerView` — the seat's own hand, the public board, the events
     7	 * addressed to it — and returns a PLAN; `applyOpponentPlan` hands that plan to
     8	 * the real engine action, which is the only thing that decides whether it was
     9	 * legal. Nothing here samples, calls the network, or reads `GameState.caseFile`.
    10	 * The LLM's contribution to an opponent's turn is voice, applied afterwards by
    11	 * the narrator to the events the turn produced (ADR-0001: the model may
    12	 * describe what happened, never decide it).
    13	 *
    14	 * The policy in one paragraph: settle a refutation we owe; accuse the moment
    15	 * the notebook leaves exactly one triple; otherwise walk toward the nearest
    16	 * room we have not yet stood in that is still a candidate answer, and once in a
    17	 * room suggest the least-tested suspect and weapon our notebook still allows.
    18	 */
    19	
    20	import {
    21	  ROOMS,
    22	  SUSPECTS,
    23	  WEAPONS,
    24	  type Card,
    25	  type Room,
    26	  type SolutionTriple,
    27	  type Suspect,
    28	  type Weapon,
    29	} from '../engine/cards.ts';
    30	import {
    31	  legalDestinations,
    32	  secretPassageFrom,
    33	  type Destination,
    34	  type Position,
    35	} from '../engine/board.ts';
    36	import {
    37	  currentPlayer,
    38	  endTurn,
    39	  getBoard,
    40	  makeAccusation,
    41	  makeSuggestion,
    42	  moveTo,
    43	  provideRefutationCard,
    44	  rollDice,
    45	  takeSecretPassage,
    46	} from '../engine/actions.ts';
    47	import { IllegalActionError, type GameState } from '../engine/types.ts';
    48	import { playerView, type PlayerView } from '../engine/view.ts';
    49	import { buildNotebook, type Notebook } from './notebook.ts';
    50	
    51	export type OpponentPlan =
    52	  | { readonly kind: 'refute'; readonly card: Card }
    53	  | { readonly kind: 'accuse'; readonly triple: SolutionTriple }
    54	  | { readonly kind: 'secret-passage' }
    55	  | { readonly kind: 'roll' }
    56	  | { readonly kind: 'move'; readonly position: Position; readonly steps: number }
    57	  | { readonly kind: 'suggest'; readonly suspect: Suspect; readonly weapon: Weapon }
    58	  | { readonly kind: 'end-turn' };
    59	
    60	/** Wide enough to cross the whole board; used to measure distances, never to move. */
    61	const BOARD_DIAMETER = 99;
    62	
    63	/** Corridor squares held by tokens other than this seat's — they block movement. */
    64	function blockedFor(view: PlayerView): Position[] {
    65	  const blocked: Position[] = [];
    66	  for (const [suspect, position] of Object.entries(view.suspectPositions)) {
    67	    if (suspect === view.character) continue;
    68	    if (position.kind === 'corridor') blocked.push(position);
    69	  }
    70	  return blocked;
    71	}
    72	
    73	/** Rooms this seat has already stood in, including the one it stands in now. */
    74	function visitedRooms(view: PlayerView): Set<Room> {
    75	  const visited = new Set<Room>();
    76	  for (const event of view.events) {
    77	    if (event.type === 'moved' && event.player === view.you && event.to.kind === 'room') {
    78	      visited.add(event.to.room);
    79	    }
    80	    if (event.type === 'secret-passage' && event.player === view.you) visited.add(event.to);
    81	    if (event.type === 'token-relocated' && event.token === view.character) visited.add(event.to);
    82	  }
    83	  if (view.position.kind === 'room') visited.add(view.position.room);
    84	  return visited;
    85	}
    86	
    87	/** True when this seat has already suggested since its current turn began. */
    88	function suggestedThisTurn(view: PlayerView): boolean {
    89	  let turnStart = -1;
    90	  for (let index = view.events.length - 1; index >= 0; index -= 1) {
    91	    const event = view.events[index];
    92	    if (event !== undefined && event.type === 'turn-started' && event.player === view.you) {
    93	      turnStart = index;
    94	      break;
    95	    }
    96	  }
    97	  for (let index = turnStart + 1; index < view.events.length; index += 1) {
    98	    const event = view.events[index];
    99	    if (event !== undefined && event.type === 'suggestion-made' && event.player === view.you) {
   100	      return true;
   101	    }
   102	  }
   103	  return false;
   104	}
   105	
   106	/** Distance in steps from `from` to every room the mover can reach. */
   107	function roomDistances(from: Position, blocked: readonly Position[]): Map<Room, number> {
   108	  const distances = new Map<Room, number>();
   109	  for (const destination of legalDestinations(getBoard(), from, BOARD_DIAMETER, blocked)) {
   110	    if (destination.position.kind === 'room') {
   111	      distances.set(destination.position.room, destination.steps);
   112	    }
   113	  }
   114	  return distances;
   115	}
   116	
   117	/**
   118	 * How badly this seat wants to be in a given room: 0 is best.
   119	 * An unvisited room that is still a candidate answer is worth most, because
   120	 * standing in it is the only way to ask about it.
   121	 */
   122	function roomPriority(room: Room, notebook: Notebook, visited: ReadonlySet<Room>): number {
   123	  const candidate = notebook.rooms.includes(room);
   124	  if (candidate && !visited.has(room)) return 0;
   125	  if (candidate) return 1;
   126	  if (!visited.has(room)) return 2;
   127	  return 3;
   128	}
   129	
   130	/** The room this seat is heading for, or null when the board offers none. */
   131	function targetRoom(view: PlayerView, notebook: Notebook): Room | null {
   132	  const visited = visitedRooms(view);
   133	  const distances = roomDistances(view.position, blockedFor(view));
   134	  let best: Room | null = null;
   135	  let bestScore: [number, number, number] | null = null;
   136	  ROOMS.forEach((room, index) => {
   137	    const distance = distances.get(room);
   138	    if (distance === undefined) return;
   139	    const score: [number, number, number] = [roomPriority(room, notebook, visited), distance, index];
   140	    if (bestScore === null || compareScores(score, bestScore) < 0) {
   141	      best = room;
   142	      bestScore = score;
   143	    }
   144	  });
   145	  return best;
   146	}
   147	
   148	function compareScores(a: readonly number[], b: readonly number[]): number {
   149	  for (let index = 0; index < a.length; index += 1) {
   150	    const left = a[index] ?? 0;
   151	    const right = b[index] ?? 0;
   152	    if (left !== right) return left - right;
   153	  }
   154	  return 0;
   155	}
   156	
   157	/**
   158	 * Where to finish this move: the target room if it is reachable now, otherwise
   159	 * the legal destination that leaves the shortest walk to it.
   160	 */
   161	function chooseDestination(view: PlayerView, notebook: Notebook): Destination | null {
   162	  const blocked = blockedFor(view);
   163	  const moves = legalDestinations(getBoard(), view.position, view.roll ?? 0, blocked);
   164	  if (moves.length === 0) return null;
   165	
   166	  const target = targetRoom(view, notebook);
   167	  if (target === null) return moves[0] ?? null;
   168	
   169	  let best: Destination | null = null;
   170	  let bestScore: [number, number, number] | null = null;
   171	  for (const move of moves) {
   172	    const remaining =
   173	      move.position.kind === 'room' && move.position.room === target
   174	        ? 0
   175	        : roomDistances(move.position, blocked).get(target) ?? Number.MAX_SAFE_INTEGER;
   176	    // Prefer the shortest walk left; break ties toward actually being in a room
   177	    // this turn, then by a stable key so the choice never wobbles.
   178	    const score: [number, number, number] = [
   179	      remaining,
   180	      move.position.kind === 'room' ? 0 : 1,
   181	      0,
   182	    ];
   183	    if (bestScore === null || compareScores(score, bestScore) < 0) {
   184	      best = move;
   185	      bestScore = score;
   186	    }
   187	  }
   188	  return best;
   189	}
   190	
   191	/** How many times this seat has already named each card in its own suggestions. */
   192	function timesNamed(view: PlayerView): Map<Card, number> {
   193	  const counts = new Map<Card, number>();
   194	  for (const event of view.events) {
   195	    if (event.type !== 'suggestion-made' || event.player !== view.you) continue;
   196	    for (const card of [event.suspect, event.weapon] as Card[]) {
   197	      counts.set(card, (counts.get(card) ?? 0) + 1);
   198	    }
   199	  }
   200	  return counts;
   201	}
   202	
   203	/**
   204	 * The least-tested card the notebook has not yet eliminated: every suggestion
   205	 * should ask something this seat does not already know the answer to.
   206	 */
   207	function leastTested<T extends Card>(
   208	  candidates: readonly T[],
   209	  all: readonly T[],
   210	  counts: ReadonlyMap<Card, number>,
   211	): T {
   212	  const pool = candidates.length > 0 ? candidates : all;
   213	  let best = pool[0] as T;
   214	  let bestScore: [number, number] = [counts.get(best) ?? 0, all.indexOf(best)];
   215	  for (const card of pool) {
   216	    const score: [number, number] = [counts.get(card) ?? 0, all.indexOf(card)];
   217	    if (compareScores(score, bestScore) < 0) {
   218	      best = card;
   219	      bestScore = score;
   220	    }
   221	  }
   222	  return best;
   223	}
   224	
   225	/**
   226	 * The next thing this seat should do, from what it is entitled to know.
   227	 *
   228	 * Throws when it is neither this seat's turn nor its refutation to settle —
   229	 * the caller has asked the wrong seat.
   230	 */
   231	export function planOpponentAction(view: PlayerView): OpponentPlan {
   232	  if (view.pendingRefutation?.yours === true) {
   233	    const card = view.pendingRefutation.options?.[0];
   234	    if (card === undefined) throw new IllegalActionError('a pending refutation must offer a card');
   235	    return { kind: 'refute', card };
   236	  }
   237	  if (view.over) throw new IllegalActionError('the game is over');
   238	  if (!view.yourTurn) throw new IllegalActionError(`it is not ${view.you}'s turn`);
   239	  if (view.phase === 'awaiting-refutation') {
   240	    throw new IllegalActionError('a refutation is pending on another seat');
   241	  }
   242	  // An eliminated seat may only refute; if play ever reaches it, it passes.
   243	  if (view.eliminated) return { kind: 'end-turn' };
   244	
   245	  const notebook = buildNotebook(view);
   246	  if (notebook.solution !== null) return { kind: 'accuse', triple: notebook.solution };
   247	
   248	  switch (view.phase) {
   249	    case 'awaiting-roll': {
   250	      // A secret passage is a free crossing — worth taking when it lands us
   251	      // somewhere we still need to ask about.
   252	      if (view.position.kind === 'room') {
   253	        const through = secretPassageFrom(view.position.room);
   254	        if (
   255	          through !== null &&
   256	          notebook.rooms.includes(through) &&
   257	          !visitedRooms(view).has(through)
   258	        ) {
   259	          return { kind: 'secret-passage' };
   260	        }
   261	      }
   262	      return { kind: 'roll' };
   263	    }
   264	    case 'awaiting-move': {
   265	      const destination = chooseDestination(view, notebook);
   266	      if (destination === null) return { kind: 'end-turn' };
   267	      return { kind: 'move', position: destination.position, steps: destination.steps };
   268	    }
   269	    case 'awaiting-action': {
   270	      if (view.position.kind !== 'room' || suggestedThisTurn(view)) return { kind: 'end-turn' };
   271	      const counts = timesNamed(view);
   272	      return {
   273	        kind: 'suggest',
   274	        suspect: leastTested(notebook.suspects, SUSPECTS, counts) as Suspect,
   275	        weapon: leastTested(notebook.weapons, WEAPONS, counts) as Weapon,
   276	      };
   277	    }
   278	    default:
   279	      throw new IllegalActionError(`no plan for phase ${view.phase}`);
   280	  }
   281	}
   282	
   283	/** Hand a plan to the engine, which is the only judge of whether it was legal. */
   284	export function applyOpponentPlan(state: GameState, plan: OpponentPlan): GameState {
   285	  switch (plan.kind) {
   286	    case 'refute':
   287	      return provideRefutationCard(state, plan.card);
   288	    case 'accuse':
   289	      return makeAccusation(state, plan.triple);
   290	    case 'secret-passage':
   291	      return takeSecretPassage(state);
   292	    case 'roll':
   293	      return rollDice(state);
   294	    case 'move':
   295	      return moveTo(state, plan.position);
   296	    case 'suggest':
   297	      return makeSuggestion(state, { suspect: plan.suspect, weapon: plan.weapon });
   298	    case 'end-turn':
   299	      return endTurn(state);
   300	  }
   301	}
   302	
   303	/** The seat that owes a refutation shows a card. Throws when none is pending. */
   304	export function refuteAsOpponent(state: GameState): GameState {
   305	  const pending = state.pendingRefutation;
   306	  if (pending === null) throw new IllegalActionError('no refutation is pending');
   307	  const view = playerView(state, pending.refuter);
   308	  return applyOpponentPlan(state, planOpponentAction(view));
   309	}
   310	
   311	/**
   312	 * Play the current seat's whole turn.
   313	 *
   314	 * Returns as soon as the turn is over, the game ends, or a refutation is owed
   315	 * by another seat — that choice belongs to whoever holds those cards, human or
   316	 * policy, and the caller decides which.
   317	 */
   318	export function playOpponentTurn(state: GameState, maxActions = 16): GameState {
   319	  if (state.over) return state;
   320	  const seat = currentPlayer(state).id;
   321	  let next = state;
   322	  for (let action = 0; action < maxActions; action += 1) {
   323	    if (next.over || next.phase === 'awaiting-refutation') return next;
   324	    if (next.turnNumber !== state.turnNumber || currentPlayer(next).id !== seat) return next;
   325	    next = applyOpponentPlan(next, planOpponentAction(playerView(next, seat)));
   326	  }
   327	  throw new IllegalActionError(`${seat}'s turn did not finish within ${maxActions} actions`);
   328	}
     1	/**
     2	 * The deduction notebook: everything ONE seat can prove about where cards are.
     3	 *
     4	 * Pure closed-world logic over a `PlayerView` — the seat's own hand plus the
     5	 * events addressed to it. No LLM, no network, no randomness: two seats holding
     6	 * the same view always reach the same notebook, which is what makes the
     7	 * LLM-driven suspects' play deterministic and replayable (ADR-0001).
     8	 *
     9	 * The refutation rule is the CORRECTED one (engine finding, run e6g6g38j). A
    10	 * refuter shows a card THEY hold, so of the three cards a refuted suggestion
    11	 * named:
    12	 *
    13	 *   - a card already proven to be in ANOTHER player's hand is excluded — the
    14	 *     refuter cannot have shown a card they do not hold;
    15	 *   - a card already proven to be the REFUTER'S OWN stays a candidate — it is
    16	 *     precisely what they could have shown;
    17	 *   - a card already proven to be in the case file is excluded — nobody holds
    18	 *     it.
    19	 *
    20	 * When exactly one candidate survives, the refuter holds it. The naive form
    21	 * ("two of the three are located, so the refuter holds the third") ignores the
    22	 * middle case and derives FALSE card locations.
    23	 */
    24	
    25	import {
    26	  ROOMS,
    27	  SUSPECTS,
    28	  WEAPONS,
    29	  type Card,
    30	  type Room,
    31	  type SolutionTriple,
    32	  type Suspect,
    33	  type Weapon,
    34	} from '../engine/cards.ts';
    35	import type { PlayerId } from '../engine/types.ts';
    36	import type { PlayerView } from '../engine/view.ts';
    37	
    38	export type Notebook = {
    39	  /** The seat this notebook reasons for. */
    40	  readonly you: PlayerId;
    41	  /** Cards whose holder is PROVEN, and who holds them. */
    42	  readonly held: ReadonlyMap<Card, PlayerId>;
    43	  /** Cards proven to be in the case file — held by nobody. */
    44	  readonly caseFileCards: ReadonlySet<Card>;
    45	  /** Suspects still possible as the case-file suspect, in deck order. */
    46	  readonly suspects: readonly Suspect[];
    47	  readonly weapons: readonly Weapon[];
    48	  readonly rooms: readonly Room[];
    49	  /** The single remaining triple, or null while any category has a choice. */
    50	  readonly solution: SolutionTriple | null;
    51	};
    52	
    53	/** One refuted suggestion as this seat saw it: what was named, and by whom it was answered. */
    54	type SeenRefutation = { readonly named: readonly Card[]; readonly refuter: PlayerId };
    55	
    56	export function buildNotebook(view: PlayerView): Notebook {
    57	  const hand = new Set<Card>(view.hand);
    58	  const held = new Map<Card, PlayerId>();
    59	  for (const card of view.hand) held.set(card, view.you);
    60	
    61	  const caseFileCards = new Set<Card>();
    62	  const refutations: SeenRefutation[] = [];
    63	  let openSuggestion: { player: PlayerId; named: readonly Card[] } | null = null;
    64	
    65	  for (const event of view.events) {
    66	    switch (event.type) {
    67	      case 'suggestion-made':
    68	        openSuggestion = { player: event.player, named: [event.suspect, event.weapon, event.room] };
    69	        break;
    70	      case 'refutation-card-shown':
    71	        // Shown to this seat (or by it): the holder is settled, no inference needed.
    72	        held.set(event.card, event.refuter);
    73	        break;
    74	      case 'suggestion-refuted':
    75	        if (openSuggestion !== null) {
    76	          refutations.push({ named: openSuggestion.named, refuter: event.refuter });
    77	        }
    78	        break;
    79	      case 'suggestion-unrefuted':
    80	        // Nobody but the suggester can hold any of the three named cards. When
    81	        // the suggester is this seat, a named card that is not in our own hand
    82	        // is held by nobody at all — it is in the case file.
    83	        if (openSuggestion !== null && event.player === view.you) {
    84	          for (const card of openSuggestion.named) if (!hand.has(card)) caseFileCards.add(card);
    85	        }
    86	        break;
    87	      default:
    88	        break;
    89	    }
    90	  }
    91	
    92	  // Fixpoint: each conclusion can narrow another refutation's options.
    93	  let changed = true;
    94	  while (changed) {
    95	    changed = false;
    96	    for (const { named, refuter } of refutations) {
    97	      const possible = named.filter((card) => {
    98	        if (caseFileCards.has(card)) return false;
    99	        const holder = held.get(card);
   100	        return holder === undefined || holder === refuter;
   101	      });
   102	      const only = possible[0];
   103	      if (possible.length === 1 && only !== undefined && !held.has(only)) {
   104	        held.set(only, refuter);
   105	        changed = true;
   106	      }
   107	    }
   108	  }
   109	
   110	  const openIn = <T extends Card>(all: readonly T[]): T[] => {
   111	    const proven = all.filter((card) => caseFileCards.has(card));
   112	    if (proven.length === 1) return proven;
   113	    return all.filter((card) => !held.has(card));
   114	  };
   115	
   116	  const suspects = openIn(SUSPECTS);
   117	  const weapons = openIn(WEAPONS);
   118	  const rooms = openIn(ROOMS);
   119	  const suspect = suspects[0];
   120	  const weapon = weapons[0];
   121	  const room = rooms[0];
   122	
   123	  return {
   124	    you: view.you,
   125	    held,
   126	    caseFileCards,
   127	    suspects,
   128	    weapons,
   129	    rooms,
   130	    solution:
   131	      suspects.length === 1 &&
   132	      weapons.length === 1 &&
   133	      rooms.length === 1 &&
   134	      suspect !== undefined &&
   135	      weapon !== undefined &&
   136	      room !== undefined
   137	        ? { suspect, weapon, room }
   138	        : null,
   139	  };
   140	}

     1	/**
     2	 * The turn state machine. Every exported action is a pure function from a
     3	 * `GameState` to the next `GameState`, throwing `IllegalActionError` when the
     4	 * action is not legal. Nothing here reads a clock, a network, or an LLM.
     5	 *
     6	 * Turn shape (standard Clue):
     7	 *   awaiting-roll  --rollDice-->        awaiting-move
     8	 *   awaiting-roll  --takeSecretPassage->awaiting-action
     9	 *   awaiting-move  --moveTo-->          awaiting-action
    10	 *   awaiting-*     --makeSuggestion-->  awaiting-action   (only from a room)
    11	 *   awaiting-*     --makeAccusation-->  next turn, or game-over
    12	 *   awaiting-action--endTurn-->         next player's awaiting-roll
    13	 */

    15	import {
    16	  isRoom,
    17	  isSuspect,
    18	  isWeapon,
    19	  type Card,
    20	  type Room,
    21	  type SolutionTriple,
    22	  type Suspect,
    23	  type Weapon,
    24	} from './cards.ts';

    26	  buildBoard,
    27	  legalDestinations,
    28	  positionKey,
    29	  secretPassageFrom,
    30	  type Board,
    31	  type Destination,
    32	  type Position,
    33	} from './board.ts';

    36	  IllegalActionError,
    37	  type GameEvent,
    38	  type GameState,
    39	  type PendingRefutation,
    40	  type Player,
    41	  type PlayerId,
    42	  type SuggestionOutcome,

    44	
    45	let cachedBoard: Board | null = null;
    46	
    47	/** The board. Built once from the static topology; identical every call. */
    48	export function getBoard(): Board {
    49	  if (cachedBoard === null) cachedBoard = buildBoard();
    50	  return cachedBoard;
    51	}
    52	
    53	export function currentPlayer(state: GameState): Player {
    54	  const player = state.players[state.currentPlayerIndex];
    55	  if (!player) throw new IllegalActionError('no current player');
    56	  return player;
    57	}
    58	
    59	export function playerById(state: GameState, playerId: PlayerId): Player {
    60	  const player = state.players.find((candidate) => candidate.id === playerId);
    61	  if (!player) throw new IllegalActionError(`no such player: ${playerId}`);
    62	  return player;
    63	}
    64	
    65	/** Where a player's token stands. */
    66	export function positionOf(state: GameState, playerId: PlayerId): Position {
    67	  return state.suspectPositions[playerById(state, playerId).character];
    68	}
    69	
    70	/** The room a player occupies, or null when they are in the corridor. */
    71	export function roomOf(state: GameState, playerId: PlayerId): Room | null {
    72	  const position = positionOf(state, playerId);
    73	  return position.kind === 'room' ? position.room : null;
    74	}
    75	
    76	/**
    77	 * Corridor squares held by suspect tokens other than `exclude`. Corridor
    78	 * squares hold one token, so these block both movement and passage.
    79	 */
    80	export function occupiedSquares(state: GameState, exclude?: Suspect): Position[] {
    81	  const positions: Position[] = [];
    82	  for (const [suspect, position] of Object.entries(state.suspectPositions)) {
    83	    if (suspect === exclude) continue;
    84	    if (position.kind === 'corridor') positions.push(position);
    85	  }
    86	  return positions;
    87	}
    88	
    89	/** Where the current player may finish, given the die they have already rolled. */
    90	export function legalMoves(state: GameState): Destination[] {
    91	  if (state.phase !== 'awaiting-move' || state.roll === null) return [];
    92	  const player = currentPlayer(state);
    93	  return legalDestinations(
    94	    getBoard(),
    95	    state.suspectPositions[player.character],
    96	    state.roll,
    97	    occupiedSquares(state, player.character),
    98	  );
    99	}
   100	
   101	/** True when the current player may swap corner rooms instead of rolling. */
   102	export function canTakeSecretPassage(state: GameState): boolean {
   103	  if (state.over || state.phase !== 'awaiting-roll') return false;
   104	  const player = currentPlayer(state);
   105	  if (player.eliminated || player.hasMovedThisTurn) return false;
   106	  const room = roomOf(state, player.id);
   107	  return room !== null && secretPassageFrom(room) !== null;
   108	}
   109	
   110	/** True when the current player may name a suggestion right now. */
   111	export function canSuggest(state: GameState): boolean {
   112	  if (state.over) return false;
   113	  const player = currentPlayer(state);
   114	  if (player.eliminated || player.hasSuggestedThisTurn) return false;
   115	  if (roomOf(state, player.id) === null) return false;
   116	  if (state.phase === 'awaiting-action') return player.hasMovedThisTurn;
   117	  if (state.phase === 'awaiting-roll') return player.movedBySuggestion;
   118	  return false;
   119	}
   120	
   121	function assertActive(state: GameState): Player {
   122	  if (state.over) throw new IllegalActionError('the game is over');
   123	  const player = currentPlayer(state);
   124	  if (player.eliminated) {
   125	    throw new IllegalActionError(`${player.id} was eliminated and may only refute`);
   126	  }
   127	  return player;
   128	}
   129	
   130	function withPlayer(
   131	  state: GameState,
   132	  playerId: PlayerId,
   133	  patch: Partial<Player>,
   134	): readonly Player[] {
   135	  return state.players.map((player) => (player.id === playerId ? { ...player, ...patch } : player));
   136	}
   137	
   138	function append(state: GameState, ...events: readonly GameEvent[]): GameEvent[] {
   139	  return [...state.events, ...events];
   140	}
   141	
   142	/** Roll one die. Standard Clue rolls two; this game rolls one (see ADR notes). */
   143	export function rollDice(state: GameState): GameState {
   144	  const player = assertActive(state);
   145	  if (state.phase !== 'awaiting-roll') {
   146	    throw new IllegalActionError(`cannot roll in phase ${state.phase}`);
   147	  }
   148	  if (player.hasMovedThisTurn) throw new IllegalActionError(`${player.id} already moved this turn`);
   149	  const [value, rng] = rollDie(state.rng);
   150	  return {
   151	    ...state,
   152	    rng,
   153	    roll: value,
   154	    phase: 'awaiting-move',
   155	    events: append(state, { type: 'rolled', visibleTo: 'all', player: player.id, value }),
   156	  };
   157	}
   158	
   159	/** Spend the rolled die moving the current player's token to `destination`. */
   160	export function moveTo(state: GameState, destination: Position): GameState {
   161	  const player = assertActive(state);
   162	  if (state.phase !== 'awaiting-move' || state.roll === null) {
   163	    throw new IllegalActionError(`cannot move in phase ${state.phase}`);
   164	  }
   165	  const target = legalMoves(state).find(
   166	    (candidate) => positionKey(candidate.position) === positionKey(destination),
   167	  );
   168	  if (!target) {
   169	    throw new IllegalActionError(
   170	      `${positionKey(destination)} is not reachable with a roll of ${state.roll}`,
   171	    );
   172	  }
   173	  const from = state.suspectPositions[player.character];
   174	  return {
   175	    ...state,
   176	    players: withPlayer(state, player.id, { hasMovedThisTurn: true, movedBySuggestion: false }),
   177	    suspectPositions: { ...state.suspectPositions, [player.character]: target.position },
   178	    roll: null,
   179	    phase: 'awaiting-action',
   180	    events: append(state, {
   181	      type: 'moved',
   182	      visibleTo: 'all',
   183	      player: player.id,
   184	      from,
   185	      to: target.position,
   186	      steps: target.steps,
   187	    }),
   188	  };
   189	}
   190	
   191	/** Cross the house by secret passage instead of rolling. */
   192	export function takeSecretPassage(state: GameState): GameState {
   193	  const player = assertActive(state);
   194	  if (!canTakeSecretPassage(state)) {
   195	    throw new IllegalActionError(`${player.id} cannot take a secret passage now`);
   196	  }
   197	  const from = roomOf(state, player.id) as Room;
   198	  const to = secretPassageFrom(from) as Room;
   199	  return {
   200	    ...state,
   201	    players: withPlayer(state, player.id, { hasMovedThisTurn: true, movedBySuggestion: false }),
   202	    suspectPositions: { ...state.suspectPositions, [player.character]: { kind: 'room', room: to } },
   203	    roll: null,
   204	    phase: 'awaiting-action',
   205	    events: append(state, { type: 'secret-passage', visibleTo: 'all', player: player.id, from, to }),
   206	  };
   207	}
   208	
   209	/** Seats in refutation order: clockwise from the suggester, excluding them. */
   210	export function refutationOrder(state: GameState, suggesterId: PlayerId): PlayerId[] {
   211	  const seat = state.players.findIndex((player) => player.id === suggesterId);
   212	  if (seat < 0) throw new IllegalActionError(`no such player: ${suggesterId}`);
   213	  const order: PlayerId[] = [];
   214	  for (let step = 1; step < state.players.length; step += 1) {
   215	    const player = state.players[(seat + step) % state.players.length] as Player;
   216	    order.push(player.id);
   217	  }
   218	  return order;
   219	}
   220	
   221	/** The cards in a hand that answer a triple, in deck order. */
   222	export function matchingCards(hand: readonly Card[], triple: SolutionTriple): Card[] {
   223	  const named = new Set<Card>([triple.suspect, triple.weapon, triple.room]);
   224	  return hand.filter((card) => named.has(card));
   225	}
   226	
   227	/** The two events a completed refutation appends, with their audiences. */
   228	function refutationEvents(
   229	  suggester: PlayerId,
   230	  refuter: PlayerId,
   231	  card: Card,
   232	): readonly GameEvent[] {
   233	  return [
   234	    { type: 'suggestion-refuted', visibleTo: 'all', player: suggester, refuter },
   235	    {
   236	      type: 'refutation-card-shown',
   237	      visibleTo: [suggester, refuter],
   238	      player: suggester,
   239	      refuter,
   240	      card,
   241	    },
   242	  ];
   243	}
   244	
   245	/**
   246	 * Name a suspect and a weapon in the room the suggester occupies.
   247	 *
   248	 * Relocates both named tokens into that room, then walks clockwise for the
   249	 * first player holding any of the three named cards. That player shows exactly
   250	 * one card privately; everyone else learns only that a card was shown, and by
   251	 * whom. "Nobody could refute" is public.
   252	 *
   253	 * When the refuter holds exactly ONE matching card there is no choice to make
   254	 * and the refutation completes here. When they hold more than one, WHICH card
   255	 * they show is theirs to decide, so the game parks in `awaiting-refutation`
   256	 * with `pendingRefutation` naming the chooser and their options, and
   257	 * `provideRefutationCard` finishes it. The engine never picks for them: a
   258	 * sampled or callback-supplied choice would make the same state plus the same
   259	 * action produce different games, and would sit outside the action log a replay
   260	 * is reconstructed from.
   261	 */
   262	export function makeSuggestion(
   263	  state: GameState,
   264	  suggestion: { readonly suspect: Suspect; readonly weapon: Weapon },
   265	): GameState {
   266	  const player = assertActive(state);
   267	  if (!isSuspect(suggestion.suspect)) {
   268	    throw new IllegalActionError(`not a suspect: ${String(suggestion.suspect)}`);
   269	  }
   270	  if (!isWeapon(suggestion.weapon)) {
   271	    throw new IllegalActionError(`not a weapon: ${String(suggestion.weapon)}`);
   272	  }
   273	  if (!canSuggest(state)) {
   274	    const room = roomOf(state, player.id);
   275	    throw new IllegalActionError(
   276	      room === null
   277	        ? `${player.id} must be in a room to suggest`
   278	        : `${player.id} cannot suggest in phase ${state.phase}`,
   279	    );
   280	  }
   281	  const room = roomOf(state, player.id) as Room;
   282	  const triple: SolutionTriple = { suspect: suggestion.suspect, weapon: suggestion.weapon, room };
   283	
   284	  const events: GameEvent[] = [
   285	    {
   286	      type: 'suggestion-made',
   287	      visibleTo: 'all',
   288	      player: player.id,
   289	      suspect: triple.suspect,
   290	      weapon: triple.weapon,
   291	      room,
   292	    },
   293	    { type: 'token-relocated', visibleTo: 'all', token: triple.suspect, to: room },
   294	    { type: 'token-relocated', visibleTo: 'all', token: triple.weapon, to: room },
   295	  ];
   296	
   297	  // The named suspect is pulled into the room; if a player moves that token,
   298	  // they may suggest from there on their own next turn without moving.
   299	  let players = state.players.map((candidate) =>
   300	    candidate.character === triple.suspect && candidate.id !== player.id
   301	      ? { ...candidate, movedBySuggestion: true }
   302	      : candidate,
   303	  );
   304	  players = players.map((candidate) =>
   305	    candidate.id === player.id ? { ...candidate, hasSuggestedThisTurn: true } : candidate,
   306	  );
   307	
   308	  let refuter: PlayerId | null = null;
   309	  let matches: readonly Card[] = [];
   310	  for (const candidateId of refutationOrder(state, player.id)) {
   311	    const candidateMatches = matchingCards(playerById(state, candidateId).hand, triple);
   312	    if (candidateMatches.length === 0) continue;
   313	    refuter = candidateId;
   314	    matches = candidateMatches;
   315	    break;
   316	  }
   317	
   318	  const settled = matches[0];
   319	  let pendingRefutation: PendingRefutation | null = null;
   320	  if (refuter === null) {
   321	    events.push({ type: 'suggestion-unrefuted', visibleTo: 'all', player: player.id });
   322	  } else if (matches.length === 1 && settled !== undefined) {
   323	    events.push(...refutationEvents(player.id, refuter, settled));
   324	  } else {
   325	    pendingRefutation = { suggester: player.id, refuter, triple, options: matches };
   326	  }
   327	
   328	  return {
   329	    ...state,
   330	    players,
   331	    suspectPositions: { ...state.suspectPositions, [triple.suspect]: { kind: 'room', room } },
   332	    weaponPositions: { ...state.weaponPositions, [triple.weapon]: room },
   333	    phase: pendingRefutation === null ? 'awaiting-action' : 'awaiting-refutation',
   334	    pendingRefutation,
   335	    roll: null,
   336	    events: append(state, ...events),
   337	  };
   338	}
   339	
   340	/**
   341	 * The refuter names which of their matching cards they show, completing a
   342	 * suggestion the engine deliberately left open. Legal only in
   343	 * `awaiting-refutation`, whose `pendingRefutation` is both the turn slot (only
   344	 * the named refuter owes this action) and the closed set of cards they may
   345	 * show. The card is shown to the suggester alone; everyone else learns only
   346	 * that a refutation happened, and by whom.
   347	 */
   348	export function provideRefutationCard(state: GameState, card: Card): GameState {
   349	  if (state.over) throw new IllegalActionError('the game is over');
   350	  const pending = state.pendingRefutation;
   351	  if (state.phase !== 'awaiting-refutation' || pending === null) {
   352	    throw new IllegalActionError(`no refutation is pending in phase ${state.phase}`);
   353	  }
   354	  if (!pending.options.includes(card)) {
   355	    throw new IllegalActionError(
   356	      `${pending.refuter} cannot show ${String(card)}: not among ${pending.options.join(', ')}`,
   357	    );
   358	  }
   359	  return {
   360	    ...state,
   361	    phase: 'awaiting-action',
   362	    pendingRefutation: null,
   363	    events: append(state, ...refutationEvents(pending.suggester, pending.refuter, card)),
   364	  };
   365	}
   366	
   367	/** What the last suggestion produced, or null if none has been made. */
   368	export function lastSuggestionOutcome(state: GameState): SuggestionOutcome | null {
   369	  let start = -1;
   370	  for (let index = state.events.length - 1; index >= 0; index -= 1) {
   371	    if ((state.events[index] as GameEvent).type === 'suggestion-made') {
   372	      start = index;
   373	      break;
   374	    }
   375	  }
   376	  if (start < 0) return null;
   377	  const made = state.events[start] as Extract<GameEvent, { type: 'suggestion-made' }>;
   378	  let refuter: PlayerId | null = null;
   379	  let card: Card | null = null;
   380	  for (let index = start + 1; index < state.events.length; index += 1) {
   381	    const event = state.events[index] as GameEvent;
   382	    if (event.type === 'suggestion-made') break;
   383	    if (event.type === 'suggestion-refuted') refuter = event.refuter;
   384	    if (event.type === 'refutation-card-shown') card = event.card;
   385	  }
   386	  return {
   387	    suggester: made.player,
   388	    suspect: made.suspect,
   389	    weapon: made.weapon,
   390	    room: made.room,
   391	    refuter,
   392	    card,
   393	  };
   394	}
   395	
   396	/**
   397	 * Name a full triple and check it against the case file. Correct ends the game;
   398	 * wrong bars the accuser from winning while leaving them in play to refute, and
   399	 * ends their turn.
   400	 */
   401	export function makeAccusation(state: GameState, accusation: SolutionTriple): GameState {
   402	  const player = assertActive(state);
   403	  if (state.phase === 'awaiting-refutation') {
   404	    throw new IllegalActionError('the pending refutation must be shown before accusing');
   405	  }
   406	  if (!isSuspect(accusation.suspect) || !isWeapon(accusation.weapon) || !isRoom(accusation.room)) {
   407	    throw new IllegalActionError(`not a valid accusation: ${JSON.stringify(accusation)}`);
   408	  }
   409	  const correct =
   410	    accusation.suspect === state.caseFile.suspect &&
   411	    accusation.weapon === state.caseFile.weapon &&
   412	    accusation.room === state.caseFile.room;
   413	
   414	  const accused: GameEvent = {
   415	    type: 'accusation-made',
   416	    visibleTo: 'all',
   417	    player: player.id,
   418	    suspect: accusation.suspect,
   419	    weapon: accusation.weapon,
   420	    room: accusation.room,
   421	    correct,
   422	  };
   423	
   424	  if (correct) {
   425	    return {
   426	      ...state,
   427	      phase: 'game-over',
   428	      roll: null,
   429	      over: true,
   430	      winner: player.id,
   431	      events: append(state, accused, {
   432	        type: 'game-over',
   433	        visibleTo: 'all',
   434	        winner: player.id,
   435	        caseFile: state.caseFile,
   436	      }),
   437	    };
   438	  }
   439	
   440	  const eliminated: GameState = {
   441	    ...state,
   442	    players: withPlayer(state, player.id, { eliminated: true }),
   443	    roll: null,
   444	    events: append(state, accused, {
   445	      type: 'player-eliminated',
   446	      visibleTo: 'all',
   447	      player: player.id,
   448	    }),
   449	  };
   450	  return advanceTurn(eliminated);
   451	}
   452	
   453	/**
   454	 * Hand play to the next player still in the running.
   455	 *
   456	 * A turn cannot simply be passed. Standard Clue obliges the player to roll and
   457	 * move, with the secret passage as the one alternative to rolling, so
   458	 * `awaiting-roll` is refused: the seat still owes the board a move. The two
   459	 * ways out of that obligation are both real turns — a player pulled into a room
   460	 * by someone else's suggestion may suggest from there instead of moving, and an
   461	 * eliminated seat owes nothing at all (`advanceTurn` normally skips it; this
   462	 * keeps a forced eliminated turn from deadlocking). `awaiting-move` may only be
   463	 * abandoned when the roll leaves nowhere legal to go.
   464	 */
   465	export function endTurn(state: GameState): GameState {
   466	  if (state.over) throw new IllegalActionError('the game is over');
   467	  if (state.phase === 'awaiting-refutation') {
   468	    throw new IllegalActionError('the pending refutation must be shown before the turn can end');
   469	  }
   470	  const player = currentPlayer(state);
   471	  if (!player.eliminated) {
   472	    if (state.phase === 'awaiting-move' && legalMoves(state).length > 0) {
   473	      throw new IllegalActionError('the rolled move must be made before ending the turn');
   474	    }
   475	    if (state.phase === 'awaiting-roll' && !player.hasMovedThisTurn && !player.hasSuggestedThisTurn) {
   476	      throw new IllegalActionError(
   477	        `${player.id} must roll and move (or take a secret passage) before ending the turn`,
   478	      );
   479	    }
   480	  }
   481	  return advanceTurn(state);
   482	}
   483	
   484	function advanceTurn(state: GameState): GameState {
   485	  const finished = currentPlayer(state);
   486	  const players = withPlayer(state, finished.id, {
   487	    hasMovedThisTurn: false,
   488	    hasSuggestedThisTurn: false,
   489	    movedBySuggestion: false,
   490	  });
   491	  const base: GameState = {
   492	    ...state,
   493	    players,
   494	    roll: null,
   495	    events: append(state, { type: 'turn-ended', visibleTo: 'all', player: finished.id }),
   496	  };
   497	
   498	  let nextIndex = -1;
   499	  for (let step = 1; step <= players.length; step += 1) {
   500	    const candidateIndex = (state.currentPlayerIndex + step) % players.length;

 succeeded in 0ms:

             stdin behind a standing 'line' listener — readline drops lines that arrive while no
             question is pending, which silently broke piped scripts (found by the process test).
  render.ts  pure view -> lines. Reads PlayerView and Notebook only, never GameState, so the
             screen cannot spill the case file or another hand.
  loop.ts    runGame(options, {io, createClient}). Events are flushed BEFORE every prompt; the
             numbered menu lists only what is legal now while the whole vocabulary stays typable,
             and an illegal word is refused by the ENGINE in its own words (no second copy of the
             rules in the UI); IllegalActionError is caught and reprinted, never thrown at the
             player. Human is seat 1; AI seats run playOpponentTurn with narration. Ends with the
             reveal and formatUsageLedger — the token-cost evidence, on quit as well as on a win.

Tests: tests/ui/ (args, loop, cli + support.ts), no mocks — real engine, real in-process vendor
over TCP, real spawned process over a real pipe. Whole repo 331 pass / 0 fail across 18 files
(265 before), `bunx tsc --noEmit` clean.

Load-bearing evidence: full offline games at 3/4/5/6 seats reach game-over from fixed seeds with a
human who never accuses (the suspects' deduction closes them); same seed + same answers replays
byte-identical; a failing vendor degrades to engine text with ONE notice and the game still ends;
live smoke against api.poe.com printed 'LLM usage: 3 calls, 3313 tokens (2668 prompt + 645
completion)' through the product surface.

Journaled, not fixed: 'save' from the item's scope is NOT built (the host directive enumerates the
loop without it and there is no --load); describeEvent's refutation sentence reads wrong for the
refuter (engine-owned); an all-failure session still prints 'No LLM calls were made' because
SessionUsage counts only completed responses (llm-owned).

    15	/** The token figures exactly as the provider reported them. Never estimated. */
    16	export interface TokenUsage {
    17	  prompt_tokens: number;
    18	  completion_tokens: number;
    19	  total_tokens: number;
    20	}

    22	export interface ChatResult {
    23	  /** `choices[0].message.content`. */
    24	  text: string;
    25	  /** The response's `usage` object, or null when the provider did not report one. */
    26	  usage: TokenUsage | null;
    27	  /** The model the response attributed itself to, falling back to the model requested. */
    28	  model: string;
    29	}

    31	export type ChatOutcome =
    32	  | { ok: true; value: ChatResult }
    33	  | { ok: false; error: LlmError };

    35	export interface ChatOptions {
    36	  /** Overrides the configured model for this call only. */
    37	  model?: string;
    38	  /** Overrides the configured timeout for this call only. */
    39	  timeoutMs?: number;
    40	  /** Extra protocol parameters merged into the request body (temperature, max_tokens, stop, ...). */
    41	  params?: Record<string, unknown>;
    42	  /** Caller-owned abort signal, honoured alongside the timeout. */
    43	  signal?: AbortSignal;
    44	}

    46	export interface ModelUsage {
    47	  calls: number;
    48	  prompt_tokens: number;
    49	  completion_tokens: number;
    50	  total_tokens: number;
    51	}

    53	/**
    54	 * Running total for one client's lifetime — TOKEN-COUNT attribution v1
    55	 * (orchestrator resolution 5rwbt08h): what is accounted for is token counts per
    56	 * model, read from each response, not dollar cost.
    57	 *
    58	 * `byModel` is keyed by the LOWERCASED model id. Poe matches model ids
    59	 * case-insensitively and echoes back whatever casing the request used, so
    60	 * keying on the raw string would silently split one model's tokens across two
    61	 * buckets and understate its share.
    62	 */
    63	export interface SessionUsage {
    64	  calls: number;
    65	  callsWithUsage: number;
    66	  callsWithoutUsage: number;
    67	  prompt_tokens: number;
    68	  completion_tokens: number;
    69	  total_tokens: number;
    70	  byModel: Record<string, ModelUsage>;
    71	}
    72	
    73	/** The attribution key for a model id: case- and whitespace-insensitive. */
    74	export function usageKeyFor(model: string): string {
    75	  return model.trim().toLowerCase();
    76	}

    78	const BODY_EXCERPT_LIMIT = 500;
    79	
    80	/**
    81	 * An OpenAI-compatible chat-completions client (ADR-0002).
    82	 *
    83	 * One transport, no vendor SDK: `POST {baseUrl}/chat/completions` with a bearer
    84	 * key, a body of `{model, messages, ...params}`, and a reply read from
    85	 * `choices[0].message.content` plus the response's own `usage` object.
    86	 */
    87	export class ChatClient {
    88	  readonly config: LlmConfig;
    89	
    90	  #calls = 0;
    91	  #callsWithUsage = 0;
    92	  #promptTokens = 0;
    93	  #completionTokens = 0;
    94	  #totalTokens = 0;
    95	  readonly #byModel = new Map<string, ModelUsage>();
    96	
    97	  constructor(config: LlmConfig) {
    98	    this.config = config;
    99	  }
   100	
   101	  /** The URL every request goes to. */
   102	  get endpoint(): string {
   103	    return `${this.config.baseUrl}/chat/completions`;
   104	  }
   105	
   106	  /** A defensive copy of the running token ledger. Mutating it cannot corrupt the client. */
   107	  get usage(): SessionUsage {
   108	    const byModel: Record<string, ModelUsage> = {};
   109	    for (const [model, totals] of this.#byModel) byModel[model] = { ...totals };
   110	    return {
   111	      calls: this.#calls,
   112	      callsWithUsage: this.#callsWithUsage,
   113	      callsWithoutUsage: this.#calls - this.#callsWithUsage,
   114	      prompt_tokens: this.#promptTokens,
   115	      completion_tokens: this.#completionTokens,
   116	      total_tokens: this.#totalTokens,
   117	      byModel,
   118	    };
   119	  }
   120	
   121	  /**
   122	   * Perform one chat completion. Throws a typed `LlmError` on any failure —
   123	   * use {@link tryChat} where a failure must degrade rather than propagate.
   124	   */
   125	  async chat(messages: ChatMessage[], options: ChatOptions = {}): Promise<ChatResult> {
   126	    const model = options.model ?? this.config.model;
   127	    const timeoutMs = options.timeoutMs ?? this.config.timeoutMs;
   128	    const body = JSON.stringify({ ...(options.params ?? {}), model, messages });
   129	
   130	    const raw = await this.#send(body, timeoutMs, options.signal);
   131	    const result = parseCompletion(raw, this.endpoint, model);
   132	    this.#account(result);
   133	    return result;
   134	  }
   135	
   136	  /**
   137	   * Perform one chat completion, returning a failure instead of throwing.
   138	   *
   139	   * PRODUCT.md hard constraint 5: the game stays playable when the model
   140	   * errors, so the game loop calls this and falls back to plain engine text.
   141	   */
   142	  async tryChat(messages: ChatMessage[], options: ChatOptions = {}): Promise<ChatOutcome> {
   143	    try {
   144	      return { ok: true, value: await this.chat(messages, options) };
   145	    } catch (error) {
   146	      if (error instanceof LlmError) return { ok: false, error };
   147	      throw error;
   148	    }
   149	  }
   150	
   151	  /**
   152	   * One HTTP exchange, with a single retry on 429/5xx.
   153	   *
   154	   * A 4xx other than 429 is the caller's own bug and is never retried; a
   155	   * timeout is not retried either, because the caller's deadline has already
   156	   * passed once.
   157	   */
   158	  async #send(body: string, timeoutMs: number, callerSignal?: AbortSignal): Promise<string> {
   159	    for (let attempt = 0; ; attempt++) {
   160	      const { response, raw } = await this.#attempt(body, timeoutMs, callerSignal);
   161	      if (response.ok) return raw;
   162	
   163	      const retryable = response.status === 429 || response.status >= 500;
   164	      const excerpt = excerptOf(raw);
   165	
   166	      if (retryable && attempt === 0) {
   167	        await Bun.sleep(this.config.retryBackoffMs);
   168	        continue;
   169	      }
   170	
   171	      throw new LlmHttpError({
   172	        status: response.status,
   173	        statusText: response.statusText,
   174	        url: this.endpoint,
   175	        bodyExcerpt: excerpt,
   176	      });
   177	    }
   178	  }
   179	
   180	  async #attempt(
   181	    body: string,
   182	    timeoutMs: number,
   183	    callerSignal?: AbortSignal,
   184	  ): Promise<{ response: Response; raw: string }> {
   185	    const controller = new AbortController();
   186	    const onCallerAbort = () => controller.abort(callerSignal?.reason);
   187	    callerSignal?.addEventListener("abort", onCallerAbort, { once: true });
   188	
   189	    let timedOut = false;
   190	    const timer = setTimeout(() => {
   191	      timedOut = true;
   192	      controller.abort();
   193	    }, timeoutMs);
   194	
   195	    try {
   196	      const response = await fetch(this.endpoint, {
   197	        method: "POST",
   198	        headers: {
   199	          Authorization: `Bearer ${this.config.apiKey}`,
   200	          "Content-Type": "application/json",
   201	          Accept: "application/json",
   202	        },
   203	        body,
   204	        signal: controller.signal,
   205	      });
   206	      // The body is read INSIDE the armed window: a vendor that sends headers
   207	      // and then stalls the stream is a timeout, not a hang (panel critique,
   208	      // drive kqrr2q4q). The same abort covers both halves of the exchange.
   209	      const raw = await response.text();
   210	      return { response, raw };
   211	    } catch (cause) {
   212	      if (timedOut) {
   213	        throw new LlmTimeoutError({ baseUrl: this.config.baseUrl, timeoutMs });
   214	      }
   215	      if (callerSignal?.aborted) throw cause;
   216	      throw new LlmNetworkError({ baseUrl: this.config.baseUrl, cause });
   217	    } finally {
   218	      clearTimeout(timer);
   219	      callerSignal?.removeEventListener("abort", onCallerAbort);
   220	    }
   221	  }
   222	
   223	  #account(result: ChatResult): void {
   224	    this.#calls += 1;
   225	    if (result.usage === null) return;
   226	
   227	    this.#callsWithUsage += 1;
   228	    this.#promptTokens += result.usage.prompt_tokens;
   229	    this.#completionTokens += result.usage.completion_tokens;
   230	    this.#totalTokens += result.usage.total_tokens;
   231	
   232	    const key = usageKeyFor(result.model);
   233	    const totals = this.#byModel.get(key) ?? {
   234	      calls: 0,
   235	      prompt_tokens: 0,
   236	      completion_tokens: 0,
   237	      total_tokens: 0,
   238	    };
   239	    totals.calls += 1;
   240	    totals.prompt_tokens += result.usage.prompt_tokens;
   241	    totals.completion_tokens += result.usage.completion_tokens;
   242	    totals.total_tokens += result.usage.total_tokens;
   243	    this.#byModel.set(key, totals);
   244	  }
   245	}
   246	
   247	/** Build a client straight from the environment and `.env.local`. */
   248	export function createChatClient(options: LoadLlmConfigOptions = {}): ChatClient {
   249	  return new ChatClient(loadLlmConfig(options));
   250	}
   251	
   252	/** Read `choices[0].message.content` and the optional `usage` object out of a raw response body. */
   253	export function parseCompletion(raw: string, url: string, requestedModel: string): ChatResult {
   254	  let payload: unknown;
   255	  try {
   256	    payload = JSON.parse(raw);
   257	  } catch {
   258	    throw new LlmProtocolError({ url, reason: "response body is not JSON", bodyExcerpt: excerptOf(raw) });
   259	  }
   260	
   261	  if (typeof payload !== "object" || payload === null) {
   262	    throw new LlmProtocolError({ url, reason: "response body is not an object", bodyExcerpt: excerptOf(raw) });
   263	  }
   264	
   265	  const record = payload as Record<string, unknown>;
   266	  const choices = record.choices;
   267	  if (!Array.isArray(choices) || choices.length === 0) {
   268	    throw new LlmProtocolError({ url, reason: "no choices in response", bodyExcerpt: excerptOf(raw) });
   269	  }
   270	
   271	  const message = (choices[0] as Record<string, unknown> | undefined)?.message as
   272	    | Record<string, unknown>
   273	    | undefined;
   274	  const text = message?.content;
   275	  if (typeof text !== "string") {
   276	    throw new LlmProtocolError({
   277	      url,
   278	      reason: "choices[0].message.content is not a string",
   279	      bodyExcerpt: excerptOf(raw),
   280	    });
   281	  }
   282	
   283	  const model = typeof record.model === "string" && record.model.length > 0 ? record.model : requestedModel;
   284	
   285	  return { text, usage: parseUsage(record.usage), model };
   286	}
   287	
   288	/**
   289	 * Read the provider's `usage` object.
   290	 *
   291	 * Absent or unusable usage yields null — tolerated, per ADR-0002, because not
   292	 * every OpenAI-compatible host reports it. A partial object is NEVER
   293	 * zero-filled (panel critique, drive kqrr2q4q — fabricated counts corrupt the
   294	 * token ledger): the third field is derived exactly when two are present
   295	 * (prompt + completion = total), and a lone field is no usage at all.
   296	 */
   297	export function parseUsage(value: unknown): TokenUsage | null {
   298	  if (typeof value !== "object" || value === null) return null;
   299	  const record = value as Record<string, unknown>;
   300	
   301	  const prompt = numberOrNull(record.prompt_tokens);
   302	  const completion = numberOrNull(record.completion_tokens);
   303	  const total = numberOrNull(record.total_tokens);
   304	
   305	  if (prompt !== null && completion !== null) {
   306	    return { prompt_tokens: prompt, completion_tokens: completion, total_tokens: total ?? prompt + completion };
   307	  }
   308	  if (total !== null && prompt !== null) {
   309	    return { prompt_tokens: prompt, completion_tokens: total - prompt, total_tokens: total };
   310	  }
   311	  if (total !== null && completion !== null) {
   312	    return { prompt_tokens: total - completion, completion_tokens: completion, total_tokens: total };
   313	  }
   314	  return null;
   315	}
   316	
   317	function numberOrNull(value: unknown): number | null {
   318	  return typeof value === "number" && Number.isFinite(value) ? value : null;
   319	}
   320	
   321	function excerptOf(body: string): string {
   322	  const trimmed = body.trim();
   323	  return trimmed.length > BODY_EXCERPT_LIMIT
   324	    ? `${trimmed.slice(0, BODY_EXCERPT_LIMIT)}… (truncated)`
   325	    : trimmed;
   326	}
   130	  return {
   131	    victim: tidy(record.victim) ?? CANNED_SCENARIO.victim,
   132	    setting: tidy(record.setting) ?? CANNED_SCENARIO.setting,
   133	    intro: tidy(record.intro) ?? CANNED_SCENARIO.intro,
   134	    personas,
   135	    source: 'llm',
   136	  };
   137	}
   138	
   139	export type ScenarioOptions = {
   140	  /** Model for this call only; defaults to the client's configured model. */
   141	  readonly model?: string;
   142	};
   143	
   144	/**
   145	 * Ask the model for one scenario, degrading to the canned one on any failure.
   146	 *
   147	 * `response_format: {type: "json_object"}` is requested because the endpoints
   148	 * that honour it produce far cleaner replies. A provider that does not know the
   149	 * parameter answers 4xx, so exactly one retry is made without it before giving
   150	 * up — a 5xx is NOT retried here, the transport already retried it once.
   151	 */
   152	export async function requestScenario(
   153	  client: ChatClient,
   154	  options: ScenarioOptions = {},
   155	): Promise<Scenario> {
   156	  const messages = scenarioMessages();
   157	  const model = options.model === undefined ? {} : { model: options.model };
   158	
   159	  const first = await client.tryChat(messages, {
   160	    ...model,
   161	    params: { temperature: 0.9, response_format: { type: 'json_object' } },
   162	  });
   163	
   164	  let reply = first;
   165	  if (!first.ok && first.error instanceof LlmHttpError && first.error.status < 500) {
   166	    reply = await client.tryChat(messages, { ...model, params: { temperature: 0.9 } });
   167	  }
   168	
   169	  if (!reply.ok) return CANNED_SCENARIO;
   170	  return parseScenario(reply.value.text) ?? CANNED_SCENARIO;
   171	}
     1	/**
     2	 * The narrator: engine events in, prose out.
     3	 *
     4	 * One batched call per turn — a call per event would be slow, expensive, and
     5	 * would lose the thread of what just happened. The engine's own
     6	 * `describeEvent` sentence for each event is what goes INTO the prompt, so the
     7	 * model is rephrasing a fact rather than inventing one, and it is also what
     8	 * comes back out on any failure: narration is discardable by construction
     9	 * (CONTEXT.md "Narration", ADR-0001).
    10	 *
    11	 * Narration is always FOR someone, and the request says who. Whatever events
    12	 * the caller hands over are run through the engine's own `isVisibleTo` before a
    13	 * prompt is built, so a caller slicing a turn off the raw `state.events` cannot
    14	 * put another player's private refutation card into a vendor request — the
    15	 * defense does not depend on every call site remembering to filter first
    16	 * (ADR-0001: prompts carry only what the receiving player is entitled to know).
    17	 */
    18	
    19	import type { GameEvent, PlayerId } from '../engine/types.ts';
    20	import { describeEvent, isVisibleTo } from '../engine/view.ts';
    21	import type { ChatClient, ChatMessage } from '../llm/index.ts';
    22	import type { Scenario } from './scenario.ts';
    23	
    24	export type NarrationLine = {
    25	  readonly event: GameEvent;
    26	  readonly text: string;
    27	  readonly source: 'llm' | 'fallback';
    28	};
    29	
    30	const MAX_LINE_LENGTH = 400;
    31	
    32	/** The engine's own account of each event — the fallback, verbatim. */
    33	export function fallbackNarration(events: readonly GameEvent[]): NarrationLine[] {
    34	  return events.map((event) => ({ event, text: describeEvent(event), source: 'fallback' as const }));
    35	}
    36	
    37	export function narrationMessages(
    38	  scenario: Scenario,
    39	  events: readonly GameEvent[],
    40	): ChatMessage[] {
    41	  return [
    42	    {
    43	      role: 'system',
    44	      content: [
    45	        'You are the narrator of a parlour-murder mystery. You are told what happened and you say it well.',
    46	        'Rules you may not break:',
    47	        '- Never add an event, a clue, or a deduction that is not in the list you are given.',
    48	        '- Never say who the murderer is, or what the murder weapon or room was. You do not know.',
    49	        '- One sentence per numbered line, in the same order.',
    50	        `Reply with a JSON array of ${events.length} strings and nothing else.`,
    51	      ].join('\n'),
    52	    },
    53	    {
    54	      role: 'user',
    55	      content: [
    56	        `Setting: ${scenario.setting}`,
    57	        `The victim: ${scenario.victim}`,
    58	        '',
    59	        'What just happened, in order:',
    60	        ...events.map((event, index) => `${index + 1}. ${describeEvent(event)}`),
    61	      ].join('\n'),
    62	    },
    63	  ];
    64	}
    65	
    66	function unfence(text: string): string {
    67	  const fenced = /^\s*```(?:json)?\s*\n([\s\S]*?)\n?\s*```\s*$/.exec(text);
    68	  return fenced?.[1] ?? text;
    69	}
    70	
    71	/**
    72	 * Read up to `count` narration strings from a model reply.
    73	 *
    74	 * Returns one slot per event: a string where the model supplied a usable one,
    75	 * null where it did not. A reply that is not a JSON array yields all nulls, so
    76	 * the batch falls back wholesale.
    77	 */
    78	export function parseNarration(text: string, count: number): (string | null)[] {
    79	  const slots: (string | null)[] = Array.from({ length: count }, () => null);
    80	  let payload: unknown;
    81	  try {
    82	    payload = JSON.parse(unfence(text));
    83	  } catch {
    84	    return slots;
    85	  }
    86	  if (!Array.isArray(payload)) return slots;
    87	
    88	  for (let index = 0; index < count; index += 1) {
    89	    const entry = payload[index];
    90	    if (typeof entry !== 'string') continue;
    91	    const line = entry.replace(/\s+/g, ' ').trim();
    92	    if (line.length === 0) continue;
    93	    slots[index] = line.length > MAX_LINE_LENGTH ? `${line.slice(0, MAX_LINE_LENGTH)}…` : line;
    94	  }
    95	  return slots;
    96	}
    97	
    98	export type NarrationRequest = {
    99	  readonly scenario: Scenario;
   100	  /** Who this narration is for. Events not addressed to them are dropped. */
   101	  readonly viewer: PlayerId;
   102	  /** Candidate events. Filtered to the viewer's before anything else happens. */
   103	  readonly events: readonly GameEvent[];
   104	  /** Model for this call only; defaults to the client's configured model. */
   105	  readonly model?: string;
   106	};
   107	
   108	/**
   109	 * Narrate a turn's events to one viewer, falling back to the engine's own
   110	 * sentences per event. Events the viewer may not see are dropped, not muted:
   111	 * they get no line, because there is nothing to tell them about.
   112	 */
   113	export async function narrateEvents(
   114	  client: ChatClient,
   115	  request: NarrationRequest,
   116	): Promise<NarrationLine[]> {
   117	  const events = request.events.filter((event) => isVisibleTo(event, request.viewer));
   118	  if (events.length === 0) return [];
   119	
   120	  const reply = await client.tryChat(narrationMessages(request.scenario, events), {
   121	    ...(request.model === undefined ? {} : { model: request.model }),
   122	    params: { temperature: 0.8 },
   123	  });
   124	  if (!reply.ok) return fallbackNarration(events);
   125	
   126	  const slots = parseNarration(reply.value.text, events.length);
   127	  return events.map((event, index) => {
   128	    const line = slots[index];
   129	    return line === null || line === undefined
   130	      ? { event, text: describeEvent(event), source: 'fallback' as const }
   131	      : { event, text: line, source: 'llm' as const };
   132	  });
   133	}
